To bridge these discrepancies, we propose WOW-Seg, a Word-free Open World Segmentation model for segmenting and recognizing objects from open-set categories. Specifically, WOW-Seg introduces a novel visual prompt module, Mask2Token, which transforms image masks into visual tokens and ensures their alignment with the VLLM feature space. Moreover, we introduce the Cascade Attention Mask to decouple information across different instances. This approach mitigates inter-instance interference, leading to a significant improvement in model performance. We further construct an open world region recognition test benchmark: the Region Recognition Dataset (RR-7K). With 7,662 classes, it represents the most extensive category-rich region recognition dataset to date. WOW-Seg attains strong results on the LVIS dataset, achieving a semantic similarity of 89.7 and a semantic IoU of 82.4. This performance surpasses the previous SOTA while using only one-eighth the parameter count. These results underscore the strong open world generalization capabilities of WOW-Seg. The code and related resources are available at https://github.com/AAwcAA/WOW-Seg-Meta.

</details>


### [74] [VGGT-Occ: Geometry-Grounded and Density-Aware Gated Fusion for 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16911)
*Xun Chen,Tianchen Deng,Rui Wang,Fangjinhua Wang,Junyi Ma,Hongming Shen,Hesheng Wang,Danwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-Occ提出了一种将几何令牌嵌入整个3D语义占用预测流程的框架，通过投影感知可变形注意力（PA-DA）和视角质量语义门控实现几何感知的2D到3D特征提升，并在SurroundOcc-nuScenes数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语义占用预测方法在2D到3D特征提升过程中仅将相机几何约束用于初始投影，后续的偏移学习、注意力加权和跨视角聚合等操作忽略了物理几何约束，导致几何信息未被充分利用。

Method: 1) 提出投影感知可变形注意力（PA-DA），将3D偏移投影回图像平面，并利用投影雅可比矩阵作为加性偏置抑制不可靠观测；2) 引入视角质量语义门控实现跨视角一致性特征融合；3) 设计由粗到细的序列化解码器，通过门控融合以信息密度分配计算量，降低解码器成本。

Result: 在SurroundOcc-nuScenes数据集上，VGGT-Occ在T=1时达到33.00% IoU和21.08% mIoU，T=2时达到33.64% IoU和21.43% mIoU，超越现有方法，且占用头仅约41M可训练参数。

Conclusion: 将几何令牌嵌入整个流程能显著提升3D语义占用预测的精度和效率，为几何感知的2D-3D特征提升提供了有效范式。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction requires accurate 2D-to-3D feature lifting, yet current methods restrict camera geometry to initial projections. Subsequent operations like offset learning, attention weighting, and cross-camera aggregation remain geometry-agnostic, ignoring essential physical constraints. We propose VGGT-Occ, a framework that embeds geometric tokens throughout the entire pipeline. We introduce Projection-Aware Deformable Attention (PA-DA) to inject geometry into all attention stages. PA-DA projects 3D offsets back to image planes and leverages the projection Jacobian as an additive bias to suppress unreliable observations. Features are then integrated through a view-quality semantic gate for cross-view consistency. To optimize both efficiency and performance, we employ a sequential coarse-to-fine decoder with gated fusion, where low-resolution features are refined into higher resolutions, allocating computation by information density while substantially reducing decoder cost. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness and accuracy of our approach. On SurroundOcc-nuScenes, VGGT-Occ achieves 33.00\% IoU and 21.08\% mIoU ($T{=}1$), and 33.64\% IoU and 21.43\% mIoU with $T{=}2$ inference, outperforming existing methods, with only ${\sim}41$M trainable parameters in the occupancy head. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [75] [HighSync: High-Quality Lip Synchronization via Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16918)
*Saeed Firouzi Daghigh,Majid Iranpour Mobarekeh,Mostafa Alavi,Mehdi Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HighSync 是一个端到端的扩散框架，用于高保真唇形同步，在512×512分辨率下原生运行，通过消除数据泄漏现象，在图像质量和同步精度上均达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有唇形同步方法难以兼顾图像质量与同步精度，图像质量好时同步精度差，反之亦然，无法满足影视等专业制作环境的高标准要求。

Method: 基于扩散的端到端框架；识别并系统性消除了数据泄漏现象，防止模型过度依赖非音频线索进行时序建模，从而迫使模型真正依赖音频信号生成同步唇部运动。

Result: 在感知质量和同步精度两个维度的综合评估中均达到最先进水平；是首个原生支持512×512分辨率的唇形同步模型，适用于影视广播等专业生产环境。

Conclusion: HighSync通过解决数据泄漏问题，成功弥合了图像质量与同步精度之间的权衡，为高质量唇形同步在专业领域的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present HighSync, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework for high-fidelity lip synchronization that generates photorealistic talking-face videos aligned with arbitrary input audio. Existing approaches consistently struggle to reconcile image quality with synchronization accuracy, producing either visually degraded outputs or temporally inconsistent lip movements. HighSync addresses both challenges simultaneously and, to our knowledge, is the first lip sync model to operate natively at 512*512 resolution, positioning it as a viable solution for professional production environments such as the film and broadcast industries. Central to our approach is the identification and systematic elimination of a data leakage phenomenon that has silently undermined temporal modeling in prior work, preventing models from developing a genuine dependence on the audio signal. Comprehensive evaluations across both perceptual quality and synchronization accuracy metrics confirm that HighSync achieves state-of-the-art performance on both fronts. Source code, pre-trained models, and supplementary video results are publicly available at: https://github.com/saeed5959/high_sync

</details>


### [76] [Motion Cues from Image-based Point Tracking for LiDAR Scene Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16922)
*Youngdong Jang,Gyeongrok Oh,Jong Wook Kim,Hyunju Ryu,Hyung-gun Chi,SeungHyeon Kim,Seungryong Kim,Jonghyun Choi,Sangpil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackCue 是一个利用点跟踪来增强 LiDAR 场景流估计中动态目标表示的自监督框架，通过图像空间轨迹提供运动线索，改进静态-动态分类标签质量，从而提升场景流学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督 LiDAR 场景流方法依赖稀疏几何观测进行静态-动态分类，容易受到数据稀疏性和遮挡的影响，产生噪声标签，提供错误的运动引导，降低了场景流学习的效果。

Method: 1) 利用点跟踪获取与 LiDAR 点关联的密集图像空间轨迹；2) 提出视觉一致性运动补偿策略，将跟踪轨迹与自车诱导刚体轨迹在图像平面比较，分离真实目标运动；3) 进行视觉运动线索提升，将补偿后的图像轨迹与 LiDAR 点关联，细化静态-动态标签。

Result: TrackCue 显著提升了动态标签的精度和 F1 分数，并在自监督场景流估计任务上取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入图像域的点跟踪线索，TrackCue 能够提供超越稀疏几何观测的运动信息，生成更准确的静态-动态分类，为场景流学习提供更可靠的监督信号。

Abstract: LiDAR scene flow estimation is essential for autonomous driving, as it provides 3D motion for each point. Self-supervised approaches use static-dynamic classification to mitigate the imbalance between static and dynamic points, deriving targeted supervision. However, existing methods rely on sparse geometric observations for this classification, making them vulnerable to data sparsity and occlusions. The resulting noisy labels provide incorrect motion guidance and degrade scene flow learning. To address this, we introduce TrackCue, a tracking-guided framework for improving dynamic object representation in LiDAR scene flow estimation. In particular, TrackCue repurposes point tracking to obtain dense image-space trajectories anchored to LiDAR points, providing motion cues beyond sparse geometric observations. Furthermore, we present a visually consistent motion compensation strategy that compares the tracked trajectories with ego-induced rigid trajectories in the image plane, effectively isolating true object motion from ego-induced apparent motion. To transfer these isolated motion cues back to the LiDAR domain, we perform visual motion cue lifting, which associates ego-compensated image trajectories with LiDAR points for static-dynamic label refinement. As a result, TrackCue produces more accurate static-dynamic classification and provides more reliable supervision for scene flow learning. Experimental results show that TrackCue significantly improves the precision and F1 score of dynamic labels, leading to performance gains in self-supervised scene flow estimation.

</details>


### [77] [Neuroscience-inspired Staged Representation Learning with Disentangled Coarse- and Fine-Grained Semantics for EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16923)
*Xiang Gao,Hui Tian,Yanming Zhu,Xuefei Yin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经科学启发的阶段式表示学习框架，将EEG视觉解码重构为阶段特定的表示分解问题，包含低层视觉表示、高层语义表示和综合信息融合三个阶段，并引入多模态双级语义学习机制和语义潜通道，在THINGS-EEG基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法主要学习单一全局EEG嵌入进行跨模态对齐，忽视了人类视觉处理的分阶段、层次化特性，限制了模型对人脑视觉信息处理过程的建模能力。

Method: 提出阶段式表示学习框架，将EEG表示学习分解为低层视觉表示学习、高层语义表示学习和综合信息融合三个阶段；引入多模态双级语义学习机制以分离粗粒度标签级语义和细粒度图像级视觉语义信息；引入语义潜通道作为从观测EEG信号生成的语义表示通道，扩展通道级语义表示空间。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG基准上，所提方法在主体依赖零样本评估中取得优越性能，在主体独立零样本评估中实现改进的精确检索；分层检索、时间累积、多图像扩展检索和消融实验进一步验证了阶段式分解和结构化语义建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模阶段性的感知、语义和综合表示为基于EEG的视觉解码提供了一种有效的神经科学启发框架。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from electroencephalography (EEG) signals remains a fundamental challenge in brain-computer interfaces and medical rehabilitation. Existing EEG visual decoding methods mainly focus on learning a single global EEG embedding for cross-modal alignment, but they largely overlook the staged and hierarchical characteristics of human visual processing. To address this limitation, we propose a neuroscience-inspired staged representation learning framework that reformulates EEG visual decoding as a stage-specific representation decomposition problem. The proposed framework organizes EEG representation learning into three complementary phases: low-level visual representation learning, high-level semantic representation learning, and integrative information fusion. To strengthen semantic modeling, we further introduce a multimodal dual-level semantic learning mechanism that separates coarse label-level semantics from fine image-level visual-semantic information. In addition, semantic latent channels are introduced as computational representation channels generated from observed visual EEG signals, expanding the channel-level semantic representation space for structured semantic abstraction and cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments on the THINGS-EEG benchmark demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance under subject-dependent zero-shot evaluation and improved exact retrieval under subject-independent zero-shot evaluation. Additional analyses, including layer-wise retrieval, temporal accumulation, expanded multi-image retrieval, and ablation studies, further support the effectiveness of staged decomposition and structured semantic modeling. These results suggest that explicitly modeling staged perceptual, semantic, and integrative representations provides an effective neuroscience-inspired framework for EEG-based visual decoding.

</details>


### [78] [P2GS: Physical Prior-guided Gaussian Splatting for Photometrically Consistent Urban Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16925)
*Kota Shimomura,Hidehisa Arai,Tsubasa Takahashi,Takayoshi Yamashita,Hironobu Fujiyoshi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P2GS提出了一种物理一致的3D高斯溅射框架，通过联合分解视图不变的线性HDR辐射场、每视角曝光参数和色调映射函数，仅从LDR图像中实现跨视角光度一致性，解决了自动驾驶场景中多相机曝光不一致导致的伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3DGS隐式假设不同视角间曝光和色调映射一致，但真实驾驶数据中多相机管道异构和户外光照动态变化违背了这一假设，导致辐射场中烘焙了曝光差异和传感器噪声，产生伪影和不一致光照，尤其影响静态背景的真实感仿真。

Method: 提出P2GS框架，联合分解视图不变的线性HDR辐射场、每视角曝光缩放因子和色调映射函数，仅从LDR图像优化；采用基于物理成像过程的统一优化策略，引入相对曝光一致性和HDR域辐射正则化。

Result: 在真实和模拟驾驶场景中，P2GS在LDR重建上匹配或超越先前方法，同时显著提升了光度一致性、可靠的曝光归一化和物理一致的光照效果。

Conclusion: P2GS通过物理一致的辐射场分解有效解决了跨视角曝光不一致问题，在保持3DGS实时渲染效率的同时提升了自动驾驶仿真场景的光度质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently emerged as a powerful explicit representation enabling fast, high-fidelity rendering, making it a promising foundation for closed-loop simulators and perception models in autonomous driving. However, conventional 3DGS implicitly assumes consistent exposure and tone mapping across views. Real driving data violates this assumption due to heterogeneous camera pipelines and dynamic outdoor illumination, baking exposure discrepancies and sensor noise into the radiance field and producing artifacts and inconsistent illumination especially in static backgrounds crucial for realistic simulation. These issues are amplified in autonomous driving, where sparse viewpoints, varying exposures, and outdoor lighting interact, while prior work mainly targets dynamic-object reconstruction and overlooks cross-view photometric consistency. To address this limitation, we introduce P2GS, a physically consistent Gaussian Splatting framework that jointly decomposes a view-invariant linear HDR radiance field, per-view exposure scales, and tone-mapping functions from only LDR images without HDR supervision. P2GS employs a unified optimization strategy grounded in the physical image-formation process, enforcing relative-exposure consistency and HDR-domain radiance regularization. This yields a radiance field robust to inter-camera illumination differences while preserving the real-time efficiency of standard 3DGS. Experiments across real and simulated driving environments show that P2GS matches or surpasses prior methods in LDR reconstruction while providing substantially improved photometric consistency, reliable exposure normalization, and physically coherent illumination across diverse scenes.

</details>


### [79] [DEVIS-GRPO: Unleashing GRPO on Dynamic Extreme View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16937)
*Yi Zuo,Huimin Wu,Lingling Li,Fang Liu,Licheng Jiao,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEVIS-GRPO是一种基于GRPO的在线策略梯度方法，通过渐进式累积小视角增量（ADEVIS策略）实现大视角运动下的轨迹可控视频生成，无需昂贵的大视角视频配对数据，并在多个数据集上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹可控视频生成方法在小视角相机运动下表现良好，但在大视角运动时性能显著下降。现有的极端视角合成方法通常需要专用的视频配对数据，标注成本高昂。

Method: 提出DEVIS-GRPO框架，核心是ADEVIS采样策略：通过逐步累积小视角增量实现大视角相机运动。该方法无需预热策略模型收集昂贵的大视角配对视频，并通过灵活配置轨迹增加采样多样性。同时设计了多层级一致性-质量奖励函数以筛选高质量样本进行模型优化。

Result: 在Kubric-4D数据集上，非遮挡区域PSNR提升21.57%，SSIM提升7.31%；在iPhone数据集上，LPIPS降低18.56%。

Conclusion: DEVIS-GRPO有效解决了大视角运动下轨迹可控视频生成的难题，通过渐进式累积策略降低了数据需求，提高了训练效率和采样多样性，在多个基准上取得了最优结果。

Abstract: Trajectory-controlled video generation has become essential for controllable video generation. While current methods perform well under small-view camera motions, they degrade significantly with large-view motions. Existing solutions for extreme-view synthesis typically require dedicated video pairs, demanding substantial annotation effort. To address these limitations, we propose Dynamic Extreme VIew Synthesis-GRPO (DEVIS-GRPO), a GRPO-based framework for trajectory-controlled video generation, the first online policy gradient method for extreme view video generation. Central to our approach is a novel sampling strategy: Accumulative Dynamic Extreme VIew Synthesis (ADEVIS), which achieves large-view camera motions by progressively accumulating small-view increments. This method delivers two key advantages: 1) enhanced training efficiency, as it eliminates the need to warm-start the policy model by collecting expensive paired large-view videos, and 2) increased sampling diversity, achieved by flexibly varying trajectory configurations. Finally, we designed a multi-level consistency-quality reward function to select high-quality samples for model optimization. Experiments on the Kubric-4D, iPhone, and DL3DV datasets demonstrate our method's superiority. On Kubric-4D, we achieve relative improvements of 21.57% in PSNR and 7.31% in SSIM over the second-best method in non-occlusion areas. On iPhone, LPIPS is reduced by 18.56%.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Point-Wise Matching: Structural Representation Alignment for Accelerating Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16949)
*Shaodong Xu,Zhendong Wang,Litong Gong,Zexian Li,Wengang Zhou,Tiezheng Ge,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: sREPA提出了一种结构化的表示对齐框架，通过强制特征图的几何关系一致性（而非逐点匹配）来加速扩散Transformer的训练并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示对齐方法（如REPA、iREPA）主要采用逐点匹配目标或隐式架构调整，无法显式建模预训练视觉基础模型中固有的空间关系几何结构，导致对视觉表示的空间拓扑信息捕捉不足。

Method: 提出sREPA框架，将对齐目标从逐元素匹配转变为对特征图关系几何的结构化约束，强制模型学习预训练特征中蕴含的整体空间布局和结构相关性。

Result: 相比现有最先进的对齐策略，sREPA实现了更快、更稳定的收敛，并提升了生成样本质量。

Conclusion: 扩散模型训练中的表示对齐应被明确建模为结构化约束，而非简单的逐点匹配，这对于捕捉视觉表征中的空间拓扑信息至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) demonstrate that aligning noisy latent states with well-trained semantic features-as pioneered by Representation Alignment (REPA)-can substantially accelerate training and improve generation fidelity. Subsequent analysis(e.g., iREPA) suggests that these gains arise primarily from transferring spatial structure contained in pre-trained vision representations. However, mostly existing alignment methods employ point-wise matching objectives or rely on implicit architectural tweaks, which fail to explicitly model the spatial relational geometry inherent in vision foundation models. We argue that such element-wise supervision is insufficient to capture the rich spatial topology of visual representations, and that effective alignment for generation should instead be formulated as an explicit structural constraint. To this end, we propose sREPA, a structural REPresentation Alignment framework to enforce consistency in the relational geometry of feature maps, rather than merely matching individual feature points. By encouraging the model to internalize holistic spatial layouts and structural correlations from pre-trained features, sREPA achieves faster and more stable convergence, along with improved sample quality, compared to state-of-the-art alignment strategies. Our code and models will be released.

</details>


### [81] [Edit-GRPO: A Locality-Preserving Policy Optimization Framework for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16951)
*Shaodong Xu,Zexian Li,Zhendong Wang,Litong Gong,Tiezheng Ge,Wengang Zhou,Bo Zheng,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Edit-GRPO提出了一种保持局部性的策略优化框架，通过解耦编辑区域和保留区域的优化信号，在提升编辑效果的同时避免全局上下文失真，有效解决了图像编辑中空间局部性保持的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的图像编辑方法将图像视为整体进行全局策略更新，导致局部编辑意图与全局优化信号不匹配，从而引发上下文失真和边界不一致等伪影问题。

Method: 提出Edit-GRPO框架，通过为编辑区域和非编辑区域分配不同的优化信号，显式解耦编辑目标和保留目标，使策略更新与编辑任务的空间结构对齐。

Result: 在多种图像编辑场景中，Edit-GRPO相比现有优化方法显著提升了局部性保持能力，同时保持了较强的编辑性能，有效抑制了上下文失真和边界不一致等常见伪影。

Conclusion: Edit-GRPO通过空间解耦的优化策略，验证了在兼顾编辑效果的同时保持局部一致性的可行性和通用性。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in image editing lies in preserving spatial locality: edits should improve targeted content without inadvertently altering surrounding regions. However, most optimization-based editing approaches treat images as holistic entities, causing global policy updates that undermine locality and introduce undesired context changes. We observe that this issue stems from a mismatch between localized editing intent and globally applied optimization signals. Motivated by this insight, we propose Edit-GRPO, preserving Locality while optimizing image editing, a locality-preserving policy optimization framework that explicitly decouples editing and preservation objectives. By assigning region-specific optimization signals to edit and non-edit areas, Edit-GRPO aligns policy updates with the spatial structure of editing tasks, enabling localized improvements while maintaining global visual coherence. This design effectively suppresses common artifacts such as context distortion and boundary inconsistency. Extensive experiments across diverse image editing scenarios demonstrate that Edit-GRPO significantly improves locality preservation while maintaining strong editing performance compared to existing optimization-based methods, validating the generality and effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [82] [Latent Action Control for Reasoning-Guided Unified Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16961)
*Fuxiang Zhai,Sixiang Chen,Yingjin Li,Shuaibo Li,Jianyu Lai,Tengjun Huang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAC通过将推理表示为生成器内部的隐式连续动作轨迹，使统一多模态模型不仅能理解视觉内容，还能将其转化为可控的图像生成，显著提升了组合性和知识驱动的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型虽能编码视觉理解与图像生成，但理解并不自动转化为控制能力——模型可以推断对象、关系或知识线索，却无法在生成图像中实例化它们。

Method: LAC将推理表示为生成器内部的隐式连续动作轨迹（角色结构化潜在轨迹），用于规划、内部视觉草稿、诊断和优化；通过先验引导的变分潜在动作对齐（从训练时渲染的语义先验、草稿图像特征和监督终止信号学习）以及Latent-Flow GRPO来对齐潜在到图像的展开过程与终端视觉反馈。

Result: 在BAGEL-7B-MoT上实例化，LAC在GenEval、WISE和T2I-CompBench上持续改进组合性和知识驱动的图像生成，在空间关系、属性绑定和世界知识敏感提示上提升最大。

Conclusion: 习得的动作轨迹被生成器消耗，表明统一生成在理解不仅被编码、而且在生成过程中被变得可操作时受益。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models can encode visual understanding and image generation within a shared backbone, yet understanding does not automatically translate into control: models may infer objects, relations, or knowledge cues but fail to instantiate them in the generated image. We propose Latent Action Control (LAC), which makes reasoning actionable by representing it as hidden continuous actions inside a unified generator. Given a prompt, LAC rolls out a role-structured latent trajectory for planning, internal visual drafting, diagnosis, and refinement, and injects these actions into the hidden stream that conditions flow-based generation, without producing reasoning tokens or intermediate images. Since such action trajectories are unobserved, LAC learns them through prior-guided variational latent action alignment from training-only rendered semantic priors, draft image features, and supervised halting signals, followed by Latent-Flow GRPO to align the latent-to-image rollout with terminal visual feedback. This provides a control path from inferred relations, bindings, and knowledge cues to the generation process. Instantiated on BAGEL-7B-MoT, LAC consistently improves compositional and knowledge-grounded generation across GenEval, WISE, and T2I-CompBench, with the largest gains on spatial relations, attribute binding, and world-knowledge-sensitive prompts. Ablations and latent interventions show that the learned action trajectory is consumed by the generator, suggesting that unified generation benefits when understanding is not only encoded, but made actionable during generation.

</details>


### [83] [OmniVL-Guard Pro: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Omnibus Vision-Language Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16962)
*Jinjie Shen,Zheng Huang,Yuchen Zhang,Yujiao Wu,Yaxiong Wang,Lechao Cheng,Shengeng Tang,Tianrui Hui,Nan Pu,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniVL-Guard Pro，一种工具增强的智能体，将视觉语言伪造检测与定位从封闭世界预测扩展到开放世界线索驱动的推理。通过引入树状结构自演化工具轨迹生成（生成FSTR数据集）和检查器引导的智能体强化学习（CGARL），在多项任务上取得SOTA性能并展现强零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言伪造检测方法局限于封闭世界范式，依赖模型自身完成验证。但自含式多模态大模型受限于有限参数知识、静态训练语料和有限感知分辨率，在动态开放世界取证中存在实际瓶颈，尤其需要外部线索的实时事件验证和需细粒度局部操作检测的伪造分割任务。

Method: (1) 工具增强智能体架构，集成实时事件搜索、局部裁剪缩放、边缘异常筛查、人脸检测、视频帧提取和SAM3分割等工具；(2) 树状结构自演化工具轨迹生成（Tree-Structured Self-Evolving Tool Trajectory Generation），通过种子引导、无引导者自演化和弱提示硬样本合成生成多样化轨迹，构建FSTR数据集；(3) 检查器引导的智能体强化学习（CGARL），提供过程级监督以惩罚答案正确但推理扭曲的情况。

Result: 在多项任务上达到最先进性能，展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 从封闭世界预测转向开放世界线索驱动的推理是突破现有伪造检测瓶颈的有效路径，工具增强结合高质量轨迹生成与过程级强化学习监督是实现这一目标的关键技术。

Abstract: Existing vision-language forgery detection and grounding methods operate under a closed-world paradigm, assuming verification can be completed by the model alone. However, self-contained MLLMs are constrained by finite parametric knowledge, static training corpora, and limited perceptual resolution, creating a practical ceiling in dynamic open-world forensics -- particularly for real-time event verification requiring external clues and forgery segmentation demanding fine-grained scrutiny of local manipulations. To address these limitations, we shift from scaling up the self-contained model toward reaching beyond it. We propose \textbf{OmniVL-Guard Pro}, a tool-augmented agent that extends unified forensics from closed-world prediction to open-world clues-driven reasoning. OmniVL-Guard Pro integrates a tool environment spanning real-time event search, local cropping and zooming, edge-anomaly screening, face detection, video frame extraction, and SAM3-based segmentation. To generate high-quality tool-reasoning trajectories, we introduce \textbf{Tree-Structured Self-Evolving Tool Trajectory Generation}, which produces diverse trajectories through seed guidance, guider-free self-evolution, and weakly-hinted hard sample synthesis, yielding the Full-Spectrum Tool Reasoning (FSTR) dataset for training. We further propose \textbf{Checker-Guided Agentic Reinforcement Learning} (CGARL), which provides process-level supervision to penalize cases where the answer is correct but the reasoning is distorted. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniVL-Guard Pro achieves state-of-the-art performance across various tasks, and exhibits strong zero-shot generalization. The FSTR dataset and code for OmniVL-Guard Pro will be publicly released at \url{https://github.com/shen8424/OmniVL-Guard-Pro}.

</details>


### [84] [SHED: Style-Homogenized Embedding Alignment for Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16973)
*Kai Gan,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHED提出了一种基于CLIP的风格同质化嵌入对齐方法，通过去除图像和文本嵌入中的领域特定风格信息，再结合推断时的领域中心加权聚合，在多个域泛化基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型在域泛化中面临图像-文本嵌入对齐的信息不对称问题：图像编码同时包含类语义和领域风格，而文本提示仅提供基本类别线索，这种不对称性阻碍了模型向未见域的泛化。

Method: 训练阶段：对图像嵌入移除各源域的领域风格中心，对文本嵌入跨多种提示模板平均并移除全局中心。推断阶段：将多种文本领域中心投影到视觉空间，通过隶属度加权聚合预测。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上达到SOTA，例如在DomainNet上比标准微调提升4.0%。

Conclusion: 通过明确解耦并去除领域风格信息，SHED有效缓解了CLIP嵌入对齐的信息不对称问题，显著提升了域泛化性能。

Abstract: Domain generalization aims to enhance model robustness against unseen domains with embedding distribution shifts. While large-scale vision-language models like CLIP exhibit strong generalization, their direct image-text embedding alignment suffers from inherent information asymmetry: images encode both class semantics and domain-specific styles, whereas text prompts primarily convey basic class cues. This asymmetry hinders generalization to novel domains in realistic scenarios. To address this, we propose Style-Homogenized Embedding alignment for Domain-generalization (SHED), a novel CLIP-based method that aligns style-homogenized embeddings instead of raw representations from encoders in CLIP. During training, SHED removes domain-specific style centroids from both image embeddings computed per source domains and text embeddings which are averaged across diverse prompt templates and stripped of a global centroid. For inference, considering the lack of target domain information, SHED projects diverse textual domain centroids into the visual space and aggregates predictions via membership weighting. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks show SHED achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming prior methods significantly (e.g., +4.0\% on DomainNet vs. standard fine-tuning).

</details>


### [85] [Rethinking the State Update Gate for Long-Sequence Recurrent 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16981)
*Kejun Ren,Lei Jin,Tianxin Huang,Lianming Xu,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无参数、零训练的标量帧级门控机制α_t∈(0,1]，通过帧间内部特征变化闭式推导得到，用于解决流式3D重建中因逐Token门控幅度受限（中位数0.31，不超过0.6）导致的长期漂移问题。该方法可视为经典SLAM关键帧选择的连续松弛版本，在六个基准上显著提升性能，包括TUM-RGBD长序列ATE降低51%、Bonn视频深度AbsRel降低12.8%，并在KITTI长序列姿态估计上超越现有方法，同时保持严格常数内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式3D重建方法（如TTT3R）中，逐Token门控存在内在幅度瓶颈（中位数0.31，不超过0.6）且几乎帧不变，导致有效记忆视野仅约3帧每状态Token，成为长序列漂移的结构性根源。作者发现现有推理时方法仅在逐Token、帧内层面调节更新，而正交的帧级问题——每帧对状态贡献的强度——被当作内容无关处理，缺失了这一关键自由度。

Method: 提出一个标量帧级门控α_t∈(0,1]，通过帧间内部特征的闭式变化推导得出，无需额外参数、无需训练、无需额外前向传播。该门控是经典SLAM关键帧选择的连续松弛版本，以内容相关的方式动态调节每帧对隐状态的贡献强度。

Result: 在六个基准（涵盖相机姿态、视频深度、3D重建，序列长达4,541帧）上验证：TUM-RGBD长序列ATE降低51%，Bonn视频深度AbsRel降低12.8%，KITTI长序列姿态估计超越LongStream和Keyframe-VO，同时保持严格常数内存和零训练成本。

Conclusion: 帧级门控弥补了现有逐Token门控在帧级自由度的缺失，有效缓解了流式3D重建中的长期漂移问题，且无需额外参数或训练，是一种实用且高效的改进方案。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction under a strict constant-memory budget hinges on how the recurrent state is updated as the stream evolves. We profile TTT3R-style per-token gates across five benchmarks and discover a structural bottleneck: the gate is intrinsically bounded in magnitude (median $0.31$; never exceeding $0.6$) and nearly frame-invariant, yielding an effective memory horizon of only $\sim$3 frames per state token, which serves as the structural origin of long-sequence drift. We trace this to a missing axis: existing inference-time methods modulate updates only at the per-token, intra-frame level, while the orthogonal frame-level question of \emph{how strongly each frame should contribute to the state} has been treated as content-independent. We close this gap with a scalar frame-level gate $α_t \in (0, 1]$ derived in closed form from frame-to-frame changes of internal features -- a continuous relaxation of classical Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) keyframe selection that requires no parameters, no training, and no extra forward pass. Across six benchmarks spanning camera pose, video depth, and 3D reconstruction at sequence lengths up to $4,541$ frames, our gate cuts ATE by $51\%$ on long TUM-RGBD pose sequences, reduces AbsRel by $12.8\%$ on Bonn video depth, and on KITTI long-sequence pose estimation surpasses both LongStream and Keyframe-VO, while retaining strictly constant memory at zero training cost.

</details>


### [86] [DreamEdit3D: Personalization of Multi-View Diffusion Models for 3D Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16990)
*Jinxin Ai,Matthias Nießner,Ziya Erkoç*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种将2D扩散模型的个性化能力扩展到3D资产编辑的方法，通过正交视图渲染、对象级分割、两阶段优化（多视角文本反转+注意力对齐，然后全微调多视角扩散模型）实现 compositional、对象级的自然语言控制3D编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D扩散模型在身份保留个性化方面已取得显著成功，但将其扩展到3D资产仍面临多视角一致性和空间控制的复杂挑战。

Method: 1) 正交视图渲染并提取对象级分割掩码以隔离语义组件；2) 两阶段优化策略：先进行多视角文本反转与注意力对齐，再全微调多视角扩散模型；3) 推理时解耦的token嵌入与编辑提示组合生成多视角一致图像，最后提升为高保真纹理3D网格。

Result: 在多种编辑场景下，该方法成功将2D个性化的灵活性迁移到3D，相比现有基线在编辑保真度和身份保留方面达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了通过自然语言对3D资产进行组合式、对象级的编辑控制，有效弥合了2D个性化与3D编辑之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: While 2D diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in identity-preserving personalization, extending this capability to 3D assets remains a significant challenge due to the complexities of multi-view consistency and spatial control. Inspired by these 2D advancements, we present a novel personalization method for text-guided 3D editing that enables compositional, object-level control through natural language. Given a 3D input, we render orthogonal views and extract object-level segmentation masks to isolate semantic components. We then learn distinct token embeddings for each component through a tailored two-phase optimization strategy: multi-view textual inversion with attention alignment, followed by full fine-tuning of multi-view diffusion model. During inference, these disentangled tokens seamlessly compose with editing prompts to generate multi-view consistent images, which are subsequently lifted into high-fidelity textured 3D meshes. Extensive evaluations across diverse editing scenarios demonstrate that our method successfully transfers the flexibility of 2D personalization to 3D, achieving state-of-the-art edit faithfulness and identity preservation compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [87] [RHINO: Reconstructing Human Interactions with Novel Objects from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17014)
*Lixin Xue,Chengwei Zheng,Georgios Paschalidis,Chen Guo,Manuel Kaufmann,Juan Zarate,Dimitrios Tzionas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RHINO是一个三步框架，从单目RGB视频中恢复3D空间中的人体、未见过的操作物体和静态场景。它利用3D感知基础模型稳定SfM，结合现成的人体估计方法，并通过组合神经场和可微接触先验优化形状，在4D重建和新视角合成上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目RGB视频中重建人、物体及其3D交互是智能系统的长期目标，但该任务因深度模糊、人-物相互遮挡、相机与物体运动耦合而高度病态。现有方法大多孤立处理人或物体，忽略二者交互，或假设已知3D形状/相机，不适用于实际应用。

Method: 三步框架：(1) 利用3D感知基础模型获取线索稳定SfM，从前景像素恢复操作物体的粗略形状和表观运动，从背景像素恢复场景形状和相机运动；(2) 用现成方法估计相机帧中的人体，从表观运动中减去相机运动提取物体运动，将人、物体和场景注册到共同世界坐标系；(3) 使用带逐分量符号距离场的组合神经场细化形状，结合可微接触先验吸引表面同时惩罚穿透，提升物理合理性。

Result: 在新数据集（手持单目视频与体积4D捕捉同步）上，RHINO在新视角合成和4D重建方面优于最先进基线，消融实验表明每个步骤均贡献显著。

Conclusion: RHINO通过三步框架有效解决了从单目视频中联合重建人、物体和场景的挑战，性能优于现有方法，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Reconstructing people, objects, and their interactions in 3D is a long-standing goal for intelligent systems. Often the input is RGB video from a moving camera, making the task ill-posed; depth is ambiguous, humans and objects occlude each other, and camera and object motion entangle to create apparent motion. Most prior work addresses humans or objects in isolation, ignoring their interplay, or assumes known 3D shapes or cameras, which is impractical for real-world applications. We develop RHINO (Reconstructing Human Interactions with Novel Objects), a three-step framework that recovers in 3D a human, novel (unseen) manipulated object, and static scene in a common world frame from a monocular RGB video. First, we leverage 3D-aware foundation models to obtain cues that stabilize Structure-from-Motion (SfM) even for low-texture regions; this yields a coarse shape and apparent motion of a manipulated object from foreground pixels, and a coarse scene shape and camera motion from background pixels. Second, we estimate a human in the camera frame via an off-the-shelf method, and subtract the camera motion from apparent motion to extract the object motion; this registers the human, object, and coarse scene shapes into a common world frame. Third, we refine shapes using a compositional neural field with per-component signed-distance fields. The latter further enables differentiable contact priors that attract surfaces while penalizing interpenetration, improving the physical plausibility of the final reconstruction. For evaluation, we capture a new dataset of handheld monocular videos synchronized with a volumetric 4D capture stage, providing ground-truth shape and camera motion. RHINO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on novel-view synthesis and 4D reconstruction. Ablations show that each stage contributes substantially. Code and data are available at https://lxxue.github.io/RHINO.

</details>


### [88] [StreamingEffect: Real-Time Human-Centric Video Effect Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17019)
*Yiren Song,Cheng Liu,Yuxin Jiang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamingEffect是一个面向直播类人中心应用的实时视频特效框架，通过将双向教师模型蒸馏为因果自回归学生模型、将采样步数从50步降至4步，并结合关键帧控制与大规模数据集VideoEffect-130K，实现了在单张H200 GPU上实时、高质量的720p视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播类人中心应用（如电商直播、娱乐、vlog）对实时视频特效生成需求强烈，但现有方法受限于缺乏合适数据和可部署的编辑模型；同时，通用视频编辑的加速蒸馏方法尚不成熟，缺少针对视频编辑的高效蒸馏研究。

Method: 采用上下文内视频编辑架构，先训练高质量双向教师模型，再将其蒸馏为因果自回归学生模型，并将采样步数从50步降至4步；引入关键帧控制机制，支持在线注入参考效果帧并通过流传播实现交互式编辑；构建了包含70K特效视频和60K编辑视频、覆盖600种特效类别的大规模数据集VideoEffect-130K。

Result: 在单张H200 GPU上实现了实时、高质量的720p视频编辑。

Conclusion: StreamingEffect通过模型蒸馏、关键帧控制和专用数据集构建，有效解决了人中心实时视频特效编辑的数据和效率瓶颈，为直播类应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Streaming video effect generation is highly desirable for live human-centric applications such as e-commerce streaming, entertainment, and vlogging, yet remains difficult due to the lack of suitable data and deployable editing models. Unlike generic video generation, this task requires real-time video-to-video editing that adds expressive effects while preserving human identity, background content, and temporal consistency. Existing acceleration efforts mainly focus on text-to-video generation, while efficient distillation for video editing remains largely underexplored. In this paper, we present \textbf{StreamingEffect}, a real-time human-centric streaming video effect framework. We adopt an in-context video editing architecture and train a high-quality bidirectional teacher, then distill it into a causal autoregressive student and further reduce sampling from 50 steps to 4 steps. We also introduce keyframe control, allowing reference effect frames to be injected online and propagated through the stream for interactive editing. To address the data bottleneck, we construct \textbf{VideoEffect-130K}, to our knowledge the largest human-centric video effect dataset, containing 70K effect videos and 60K editing videos across 600 effect categories curated from short-video and editing platforms. Experiments show that our method enables real-time, high-quality 720p video editing on a single H200 GPU.

</details>


### [89] [EPIC-Bench: A Perception-Centric Benchmark for Fine-Grained Embodied Visual Grounding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17070)
*Haozhe Shan,Xiancong Ren,Han Dong,Haoyuan Shi,Yingji Zhang,Jiayu Hu,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Bin Shen,Lizhen Qu,Zenglin Xu,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EPIC-Bench基准，通过6.6k精细标注的(图像,文本,掩码)三元组和23个细粒度任务，系统评估VLM在具身环境中的视觉感知能力，发现当前模型在复杂视觉-文本对齐、多目标计数、部分-整体关系理解和功能区域检测方面存在瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多采用问答或选择题形式，允许模型利用语言先验而非真正视觉接地，无法可靠评估VLM在具身智能中的视觉感知能力。

Method: 构建EPIC-Bench数据集，包含6.6k精细标注的(图像,文本,掩码)三元组，覆盖具身交互管线的三个核心阶段（目标定位、导航、操作）下的23个细粒度任务，对超过89个主流VLM进行系统评测。

Result: 先进推理模型表现有潜力，但当前VLM普遍在复杂视觉-文本对齐用于物理交互上存在困难；关键瓶颈在于多目标计数、部分-整体关系理解和功能区域检测。

Conclusion: EPIC-Bench为推进下一代视觉驱动具身模型提供了稳健基础和可操作的洞见。

Abstract: While large vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly adopted as the perceptual backbone for embodied agents, existing benchmarks often rely on question-answering or multiple-choice formats. These protocols allow models to exploit linguistic priors rather than demonstrating genuine visual grounding. To address this, we present EPIC-Bench, Embodied PerceptIon BenChmark, a fine-grained grounding benchmark designed to systematically evaluate the visual perceptual capabilities of VLMs in real-world embodied environments. Comprising 6.6k meticulously annotated tuples (Image, Text, Mask), EPIC-Bench spans 23 fine-grained tasks across three core stages of the embodied interaction pipeline: Target Localization, Navigation, and Manipulation. Extensive evaluations of over 89 leading VLMs reveal that while advanced reasoning models show promise, current VLMs universally struggle with complex visual-text alignment for physical interactions. Specifically, models exhibit critical bottlenecks in multi-target counting, part-whole relationship understanding, and affordance region detection. EPIC-Bench provides a robust foundation and actionable insights for advancing the next generation of vision-driven embodied models.

</details>


### [90] [The Learnability Gap in Medical Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17087)
*Mischa Dombrowski,Felix Nützel,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现并形式化了医学影像生成式数据增强中的"可学习性差距"：大规模预训练自编码器的潜空间虽然能无损保留判别性特征，但其结构不利于分类器学习；这种差距在多种架构、初始化策略和超参数下普遍存在，且医学领域微调无法消除。作者提出带FiLM层的噪声条件潜分类器和图像空间蒸馏方法，实现64倍吞吐量和120倍内存增益，并揭示潜空间的结构而非保真度或领域特异性是阻碍合成数据性能提升的主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于潜在扩散模型的生成式数据增强方法在医学影像类别不平衡问题上，过度关注感知 fidelity 和领域特化的自编码器微调，忽略了更根本的瓶颈——潜空间的可学习性差距，即自编码器虽能无损编码判别特征，但其潜在表示结构却不利于分类器学习。

Method: 1) 在5种自编码器家族和4个医学基准（胸部X光、皮肤镜、CT、超声心动图）上系统验证可学习性差距的存在及其普遍性；2) 开发带FiLM层的噪声条件潜分类器，结合图像空间蒸馏来探测和部分缩小该差距。

Result: 可学习性差距在多种架构、初始化策略和超参数下持续存在，医学领域微调无法消除；所提方法实现了64倍吞吐量和120倍内存增益。潜空间的结构而非保真度或领域特异性是主要障碍。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个评估自编码器潜空间的新框架，并指出潜空间的结构是缩小真实与合成医学训练数据性能差距的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Generative data augmentation with latent diffusion models is a promising strategy for addressing class imbalance in medical imaging, yet current approaches focus on perceptual fidelity and domain-specific autoencoder fine-tuning while neglecting a more fundamental bottleneck. We identify and formalize the learnability gap: large-scale pretrained autoencoders faithfully encode discriminative features for medical classification, as evidenced by near-lossless performance in reconstruction space, yet their latent representations are structured in ways that are difficult for classifiers to learn from. Across five autoencoder families and four medical benchmarks spanning chest radiography, dermatoscopy, computed tomography, and echocardiography, we show that this gap persists regardless of architecture, initialization strategy, or hyperparameter tuning, and that medical-domain fine-tuning of the autoencoder does not close it. To probe and partially narrow the gap, we develop noise-conditioned latent classifiers with FiLM layers and image-space distillation that offer 64x throughput and 120x memory gains over image-space models while serving as diagnostic tools for latent space quality. Our analysis provides a new framework for evaluating autoencoder latent spaces and identifies their structure, rather than their fidelity or domain specificity, as the primary obstacle to closing the performance gap between real and synthetic medical training data.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

### [91] [The Scaling Laws of Skills in LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16508)
*Charles Chen,Qiming Yu,Yuhang Gu,Zhuoye Huang,Hanjing Li,Hongyu Liu,Simin Liu,Jinhao Liu,Dengyun Peng,Jiangyi Wang,Zheng Yan,Fanqing Meng,Ethan Qin,Carl Che,Mengkang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体系统中技能库规模与路由/执行性能之间的缩放规律，发现路由精度随库大小对数衰减（R²>0.97），执行效果受路由影响呈乘法关系；通过单一参数b耦合两定律，优化后路由准确率从71.3%提升至91.7%，下游执行通过率提升至61.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体系统规模扩大，技能库不断积累，但技能库的缩放规律尚未被系统理解，研究者希望通过大规模实验揭示路由和执行性能随技能库规模变化的定律。

Method: 基于15个前沿大语言模型、1,141个真实世界技能和超过300万次路由/执行决策，通过实证分析识别出路由定律（单步路由精度随库大小对数衰减）和执行定律（联合路由近似乘法，正确执行可改善下游决策约4倍），并发现单一参数b耦合两定律。

Result: 路由定律R²>0.97；路由优化使准确率从71.3%提升至91.7%，劫持率从22.4%降至4.1%；ClawBench通过率从49.3%提升至61.6%，ClawMark从28.4%提升至34.5%。

Conclusion: 智能体性能不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于技能库的结构、粒度和暴露策略；路由对数衰减斜率b是连接路由侧和执行侧的核心参数，为系统优化提供了可操作的理论指导。

Abstract: As agent systems scale, skills accumulate into large reusable libraries, yet their scaling laws remain poorly understood. Across 15 frontier LLMs, 1,141 real-world skills, and over 3M routing or execution decisions, we identify two coupled laws. Routing law: single-step routing accuracy decays logarithmically with library size ($R^2{>}0.97$ for all models), with errors progressing from local skill competition to cross-family drift and capture by overly general "black-hole skills". Execution law: before state realization, joint routing is approximately multiplicative, whereas correct execution can improve difficult downstream decisions by about $4{\times}$. A single parameter, the routing logarithmic decay slope $b$, couples the two laws: routing-side fits predict execution-side rescue across models, showing that the same library property controls both pre-execution collapse and downstream recoverability. The laws are actionable: law-guided optimization raises held-out routing accuracy from 71.3% to 91.7%, reduces hijack from 22.4% to 4.1%, and transfers directionally to downstream ClawBench and ClawMark execution settings, improving mean pass rate from 49.3% to 61.6% on ClawBench and from 28.4% to 34.5% on ClawMark. These results show that agent performance depends not only on model capability, but also on the structure, granularity, and exposure policy of the skill library.

</details>


### [92] [PQR: A Framework to Generate Diverse and Realistic User Queries that Elicit QA Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16551)
*Yunan Lu,Luigi Liu,Omar Yahia,Arpit Sharma,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PQR是一个通过双模块迭代交互（查询精炼+提示精炼）自动生成既真实又触发智能体失败的查询的框架，在电商QA智能体上相比基线方法多发现23%-78%的无帮助响应，且生成查询更多样、更真实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注对抗性用户触发的智能体失败，忽视了具有真实用户意图的查询也能触发失败；且人工设计真实测试场景成本高昂。

Method: 提出PQR框架，包含两个互补模块：(1) 查询精炼模块，通过重写探索多样查询变体；(2) 提示精炼模块，利用先前反馈推导新的违反目标策略和真实性策略，用于精炼提示，进而生成触发失败且真实的查询。两模块迭代交互。

Result: 在电商QA智能体上检测无帮助响应，相比基线方法多发现23%-78%的无帮助响应，且生成的查询在多样性和真实性上均优于先前方法。

Conclusion: PQR能够有效自动发现与真实用户意图一致的智能体失败案例，为评估LLM智能体提供更高效、更真实的测试方案。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM-based agents remains challenging because identifying meaningful failure cases often requires substantial human effort to design realistic test scenarios. Prior works primarily focus on automatically discovering agent failures induced by adversarial users, while overlooking queries with real user intents that also trigger agent failures. We introduce PQR, a framework that not only surfaces agent failures with respect to specific objectives (e.g., helpfulness, safety, etc.) but also resembles real users' intents. PQR operates through an iterative interaction between two complementary modules. The query refinement module performs rewrites to explore diverse query variations, while the prompt refinement module uses prior feedback to derive new objective-violating strategies and realism policies for refining prompts, which in turn generate failure-triggering yet realistic queries. We evaluate PQR on detecting an e-commerce QA agent's unhelpful responses. Our method uncovers 23% - 78% more unhelpful responses, and our generated queries are more diverse and realistic compared to previous methods.

</details>


### [93] [Scaling Accessible Mathematics on arXiv: HTML Conversion and MathML 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16562)
*Deyan Ginev,Brian Caruso,Bruce Miller,Jeff Sank,Jacob Weiskoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了arXiv HTML论文服务的最新进展，包括社区驱动的错误修复（已解决约6000份报告中的一半）、目标90%无错误HTML的语料库转换（目前达75%）、MathML 4意图标注辅助语音输出、以及将LaTeXML移植到Rust以降低计算成本并加快预览速度。该项目仍处于实验阶段，但正在逐步成熟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv旨在提升TeX/LaTeX提交论文的HTML呈现质量，以满足读者对可访问性（如语音输出）和现代阅读体验的需求，同时降低计算成本并加快预览速度。

Method: 通过社区驱动的错误报告与修复提升HTML保真度；进行语料库规模的转换工作提升无错误HTML覆盖率；引入MathML 4 Intent标注增强语音输出的可访问性；将LaTeXML从Perl移植到Rust以优化计算效率。

Result: 约6000份用户报告中约一半已解决；当前75%的HTML转换无错误（目标90%）；实现了初步的MathML 4 Intent标注；Rust移植版本正在开发中，可降低计算成本并加快预览速度。

Conclusion: arXiv HTML论文项目仍处于实验阶段，但通过社区反馈、技术标准更新（MathML 4）和编程语言优化（Rust移植），正在逐步走向成熟，更好地满足读者需求。

Abstract: We report on the ongoing development of arXiv's HTML Papers offering, available on every new TeX/LaTeX submission since its initial release in 2023.
  The main highlights from 2025 and early 2026 are:
  (i) community-driven improvements to HTML fidelity and service health, with roughly half of 6,000 user reports resolved;
  (ii) corpus-scale conversion work aimed at 90% error-free HTML (currently 75%);
  (iii) initial MathML 4 Intent annotations for accessible speech output;
  (iv) an in-progress Rust port of LaTeXML, reducing compute costs and enabling faster previews on submission.
  The arXiv HTML Papers project remains experimental, but is gradually maturing as we better understand the needs of arXiv's readers and the technical opportunities presented by new standards and by advances in programming languages and AI.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Sentiment Classification: A Generative Framework for Emotion Intensity Evaluation in Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16613)
*Francesco A. Fabozzi,Dasol Kim,William N. Goetzmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从"识别"转向"评估"的情感建模方法，通过构建情感强度数据集并微调开源生成式语言模型输出0-100连续值，在金融等领域实现了比分类基线更优的表达力、泛化能力和迁移效果，主张情感强度评估应替代传统分类范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散情感分类在金融等应用领域存在局限性，无法捕捉情感内容的程度差异，而情感强度（degree of emotional content）对实际决策至关重要，因此需要一种更具表现力的连续值评估框架。

Method: 构建情感强度评分数据集，对开源生成式语言模型进行微调，使其能够输出0-100范围内的连续情感强度值，替代传统的离散类别预测。

Result: 该方法不仅超越了分类基线的性能，还展现出令人惊讶的泛化能力，并能迁移到情感（sentiment）和唤醒度（arousal）等相关构念上。

Conclusion: 情感强度评估应作为分类范式的替代方案，能更好地服务于金融等领域中情感程度对解释和决策至关重要的应用场景，推动NLP的跨学科重新语境化。

Abstract: We introduce a novel approach to emotion modeling that shifts the focus from
  identification to evaluation, addressing the limitations of discrete classification in
  applied domains such as finance. By constructing a dataset of emotional intensity
  scores and fine-tuning open-weight generative language models to output continuous
  values from 0-100, we demonstrate a more expressive, generalizable framework for
  sentiment and emotion analysis. Our findings not only outperform classification
  baselines but also reveal surprising generalization capabilities and transfer effects
  to related constructs such as sentiment and arousal. This work contributes to the
  interdisciplinary recontextualization of NLP by introducing emotion intensity
  evaluation as an alternative to classification, arguing that this shift better aligns
  with the needs of domains--such as finance--where the degree of emotional content is
  central to interpretation and decision-making.

</details>


### [95] [SKG-Eval: Stateful Evaluation of Multi-Turn Dialogue via Incremental Semantic Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16650)
*Avijit Shil,Suman Samui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SKG-Eval框架，通过语义知识图谱对多轮对话进行结构化建模，利用局部相关性、历史一致性和逻辑连贯性三种信号进行可解释、准确定性的对话评估，在多个基准上取得更优的人类相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评估方法（包括LLM-as-a-judge和基于嵌入的指标）依赖平面或单轮孤立的表示，难以检测多轮对话中的长程问题（如矛盾、主题漂移、实体不一致）。

Method: 将对话建模为不断演化的语义知识图谱（SKG），通过结构化三元组提取增量更新图谱，计算三个互补信号：(i)局部相关性——对齐当前提示；(ii)历史一致性——通过图谱和嵌入信号评估新信息与先前上下文的连接；(iii)逻辑连贯性——通过几何矛盾引擎检测跨轮冲突。信号通过自适应融合和近期加权趋势分析聚合成长度不变的会话分数。

Result: 在多个基准上，SKG-Eval与人类判断的相关性更高，显著提升了对扩展对话中长程不一致的检测能力，并能生成显式的矛盾证明和确定性分数。

Conclusion: 通过语义知识图谱的结构化外部状态跟踪为基于LLM的隐式推理对话评估提供了一种可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Evaluating multi-turn dialogue systems remains challenging because response quality depends not only on the current prompt, but also on previously established entities, claims, and conversational commitments. Existing automatic evaluators, including LLM-as-a-judge frameworks and embedding-based metrics, largely rely on flat or turn-isolated representations, making them less effective at detecting long-range issues such as contradiction, topic drift, and entity inconsistency. To address this, we propose SKG-Eval, a quasi-deterministic and interpretable framework that models dialogue as an evolving Semantic Knowledge Graph (SKG) of entities, relations, and commitments across turns. The framework incrementally updates the graph through structured triple extraction and computes three complementary signals: (i) local relevance, measuring alignment with the current prompt and optional reference; (ii) historical consistency, evaluating how newly introduced information connects to prior conversational context using graph-based and embedding-driven signals; and (iii) logical coherence, assessed by a geometric contradiction engine that detects cross-turn conflicts without relying on NLI models or LLM judges. These signals are adaptively fused and aggregated into a length-invariant session score via recency-weighted trend analysis. Across multiple benchmarks, SKG-Eval achieves higher correlation with human judgments and substantially improves detection of long-range inconsistencies in extended conversations. In addition, the framework produces explicit contradiction certificates and deterministic scores for fixed inputs, enabling reproducible and auditable evaluation. Overall, our results suggest that structured externalized state tracking through semantic knowledge graphs provides a scalable alternative to implicit reasoning in LLM-based dialogue evaluators.

</details>


### [96] [A Scalable Tool for Measuring Manner and Result Verbs in Developmental Language Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16654)
*Divyesh Pratap Singh,Dakshesh Gusain,Federica Bulgarelli,Alison Eisel Hendricks,John Beavers,Nathan M. Beers,Ifeoma Nwogu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型和RoBERTa分类器的计算方法，用于在句子上下文中自动识别方式动词和结果动词，覆盖率从VerbNet扩展到436个类别，平均准确率达89.6%，为发展语言学等领域的动词语义研究提供了可扩展的测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方式动词与结果动词的区分在发展语言学中对早期动词习得研究具有重要意义，但目前缺乏大规模的标注资源来支持这一区分的测量，现有标注数据覆盖有限，难以在更大范围内开展相关研究。

Method: 采用基于语言学提示的大语言模型生成句子级标注，覆盖MASC和InterCorp数据集，将VerbNet中已有的标注扩展至436个类别；然后训练RoBERTa分类器，并在三个保留的金标准数据集（包括已有标注和新专家标注集）上进行评估。

Result: 模型在多个评估集上表现良好，平均准确率最高达89.6%。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为支持发展语言学及其他语言数据集中动词语义研究的可扩展测量工具，但在边界案例、混合方式/结果动词以及下游发展应用方面仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Manner and result verbs encode different aspects of event structure and have been discussed in developmental work as a potentially informative distinction for studying early verb learning. However, this distinction remains difficult to measure at scale because large annotated resources for manner and result classification are not currently available. We present a computational approach for identifying manner and result verbs in sentence context. Using linguistically informed prompts, we generate sentence-level annotations with large language models over data drawn from MASC and InterCorp, extending coverage from previously annotated portions of VerbNet to 436 classes. We then train a RoBERTa-based classifier on these annotations and evaluate it on three held-out gold-standard datasets, including previously annotated items and a new expert-annotated set. Across these evaluations, the model shows promising performance, with average accuracy up to 89.6%. We present this work as a scalable measurement tool that can support future research on verb semantics in developmental and other language datasets, while noting that further validation is needed for borderline cases, mixed manner/result verbs, and downstream developmental applications.

</details>


### [97] [Language Acquisition Device in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16758)
*Masato Mita,Taiga Someya,Ryo Yoshida,Yohei Oseki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出受语言习得装置(LAD)启发的预预训练(PPT)方法，使用MP-STRUCT形式语言进行训练，该语言编码层次结构、特征依赖和长距离位移。500步PPT即可匹配强基线，且赋予模型对人类不合理的语言结构(如REVERSE)的抵抗性。研究发现有效的PPT语言不仅取决于层次表达性，还取决于依赖解析的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs的数据效率远低于人类。先前工作提出使用形式语言进行预预训练(PPT)来弥补这一差距，但何种形式语言最有效尚不明确。受LAD假说启发，研究者希望找到能更好模拟人类语言习得先天约束的PPT语言。

Method: 提出MP-STRUCT形式语言，包含MERGE(层次组合)、AGREE(特征依赖)和MOVE(长距离位移)三种操作。在标准Transformer上进行500步的PPT，然后在下游任务上评估token效率和结构合理性偏好。

Result: 1) MP-STRUCT的PPT在token效率上与强形式语言基线(如k-Shuffle Dyck)相当；2) 赋予模型对人类不合理的语言结构(如REVERSE)的抵抗性；3) MP-STRUCT CORE(简化版)虽无法在C-RASP中定义，但性能优于k-Shuffle Dyck，挑战了先前假设；4) 功能标记(functional landmarks)是关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 有效的PPT语言设计不仅取决于层次表达性(formal expressivity)，还取决于依赖解析的可及性(accessibility of dependency resolution)。功能标记通过降低依赖解析的歧义性来提升PPT效果。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain substantially less data-efficient than humans. Pre-pretraining (PPT) on synthetic languages has been proposed to close this gap, with prior work emphasizing highly expressive formal languages such as $k$-Shuffle Dyck. Inspired by the Language Acquisition Device (LAD) hypothesis, which posits that innate constraints preemptively restrict the learner's hypothesis space to natural-language-like structure, we propose LAD-inspired PPT: pre-pretraining on MP-STRUCT, a formal language whose strings encode hierarchical composition, feature-based dependencies, and long-distance displacement via MERGE, AGREE, and MOVE. A brief 500-step PPT with MP-STRUCT matches strong formal-language baselines in token efficiency while additionally imparting a human-like resistance to structurally implausible languages (e.g., REVERSE). Analyzing simplified variants, we find that MP-STRUCT CORE outperforms $k$-Shuffle Dyck despite not being definable in C-RASP (a formal bound on transformer expressivity), challenging the prior hypothesis that effective PPT languages must be both hierarchically expressive and circuit-theoretically learnable. We show that functional landmarks, which reduce dependency resolution ambiguity, are a key driver, suggesting that effective PPT design depends not only on expressivity but also on the accessibility of dependency resolution.

</details>


### [98] [Retrieval-Based Multi-Label Legal Annotation: Extensible, Data-Efficient and Hallucination-Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16767)
*Li Zhang,Jaromir Savelka,Kevin Ashley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将多标签法律标注任务转化为检索问题，使用冻结的检索模型嵌入文档和标签描述，通过k近邻预测标签，避免了参数化编码器的重新训练和生成式大模型的幻觉问题。在三个法律数据集上，检索方法在准确率、数据效率和计算成本方面均优于对比方法，且天然消除了标签外幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签法律标注面临标签空间大且不断演化、文档冗长、监督信号有限等挑战。传统参数化编码器在标签集变化时需要重新训练，而生成式大模型在标签空间增大时性能下降且成本高昂，亟需一种高效、可扩展且无需重新训练的替代方案。

Method: 将法律标注建模为检索问题：使用冻结的检索模型（如Qwen-8B）将文档和标签描述嵌入同一语义空间，通过k近邻搜索预测标签。标签集更新时仅需重新嵌入和索引标签描述，无需梯度回传或重新训练。

Result: 在ECtHR-A、ECtHR-B和Eurlex（100标签）三个数据集上，检索方法取得了竞争性准确率和强数据效率。Eurlex上Macro-F1从40.41（GPT-5.2零样本）提升至49.12，计算成本降低20-30倍；仅用100个样本时，ECtHR-A上Micro-F1近乎翻倍（48.29 vs. 27.87）。GPT-5.2在确定性解码下产生0.12-0.9%的标签外幻觉，而检索方法严格遵循定义标签集，消除了幻觉。

Conclusion: 基于检索模型的标注器是高基数、快速变化的法律标签空间中实用且可部署的替代方案，兼具高准确率、数据效率、计算成本和零幻觉优势。

Abstract: Multi-label legal annotation requires assigning multiple labels from large, evolving taxonomies to long, fact-intensive documents, often under limited supervision. Parametric encoders typically require task-specific training and retraining when the label set changes, while prompting generative large language models becomes costly and degrades as the label space grows. We cast legal annotation as retrieval: we embed documents and label descriptions with a frozen retrieval model and predict labels via k-nearest neighbors in the embedding space, enabling updates by re-embedding and re-indexing rather than gradient-based backpropagation. Across three legal datasets (ECtHR-A, ECtHR-B, and Eurlex with 100 labels), retrieval achieves competitive accuracy and strong data efficiency; on Eurlex, Qwen-8B retrieval improves Macro-F1 from 40.41 (GPT-5.2, zero-shot) to 49.12 while reducing estimated compute by 20-30 times compared to fine-tuning. With only (N=100) training samples, retrieval nearly doubles Micro-F1 over hierarchical Legal-BERT on ECtHR-A (48.29 vs. 27.87). We also quantify a reliability failure mode of generative inference: GPT-5.2 hallucinates labels outside the provided taxonomy in 0.12-0.9% of test samples under deterministic decoding. In contrast, retrieval strictly respects defined label sets, eliminating hallucination by design. These results suggest retrieval-model-based annotators are a practical, deployable alternative for high-cardinality and rapidly changing legal label spaces.

</details>


### [99] [AgentKernelArena: Generalization-Aware Benchmarking of GPU Kernel Optimization Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16819)
*Sharareh Younesian,Wenwen Ouyang,Sina Rafati,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Sharon Zhou,Ji Liu,Yue Liu,Yuchen Yang,Hao Li,Ziqiong Liu,Dong Li,Vikram Appia,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgentKernelArena 是一个用于评估 AI 编码智能体在 GPU 内核优化任务上表现的基准测试平台，包含 196 个任务，涵盖 HIP-to-HIP、Triton-to-Triton 优化和 PyTorch-to-HIP 翻译，并引入未见配置泛化测试。实验表明最强配置在三大任务类别上分别实现最高 6.89x、6.69x 和 2.13x 的加速，但 PyTorch-to-HIP 任务在泛化测试中正确率大幅下降，暴露出智能体硬编码形状假设的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GPU 内核基准测试仅评估单次 LLM 调用，而非完整的智能体迭代工作流程；同时缺乏内核间优化和未见配置泛化测试。因此需要一个新的基准来系统评估 AI 编码智能体在 GPU 内核优化中的实际能力。

Method: 构建包含 196 个任务的三类优化任务（HIP-to-HIP、Triton-to-Triton、PyTorch-to-HIP）的基准平台；采用隔离工作区、门控编译、正确性和性能检查、集中评分机制；设计未见配置泛化协议，测试优化结果是否能迁移到智能体未观察过的输入配置上。在 Cursor Agent、Claude Code、Codex Agent 等生产级智能体上进行了实验评估。

Result: 最强配置在 PyTorch-to-HIP 上实现平均 6.89x 加速，HIP-to-HIP 上 6.69x 加速，Triton-to-Triton 上 2.13x 加速；编译几乎完美，大多数任务类别正确率高。在未见配置泛化测试中，HIP-to-HIP 和 Triton-to-Triton 泛化良好，但 PyTorch-to-HIP 正确率显著下降。

Conclusion: AgentKernelArena 可作为模块化、可扩展的框架，用于严格评估智能体 GPU 内核优化能力。当前智能体在翻译生成任务（PyTorch-to-HIP）中存在过度拟合输入形状的局限，而在同类优化任务中泛化能力较强。

Abstract: GPU kernel optimization is increasingly critical for efficient deep learning systems, but writing high-performance kernels still requires substantial low-level expertise. Recent AI coding agents can iteratively read code, invoke compilers and profilers, and refine implementations, yet existing kernel benchmarks evaluate single LLM calls rather than full agent workflows, and none include both kernel-to-kernel optimization and unseen-configuration generalization testing. We present AgentKernelArena, an open-source benchmark for measuring AI coding agents on GPU kernel optimization. The benchmark contains 196 tasks spanning HIP-to-HIP optimization, Triton-to-Triton optimization, and PyTorch-to-HIP translation, and evaluates complete agent workflows in isolated workspaces using gated compilation, correctness, and performance checks, centralized scoring and an unseen-configuration generalization protocol that tests whether optimizations transfer to input configurations the agent never observed. Across production agents including Cursor Agent, Claude Code, and Codex Agent, we find near-perfect compilation and high correctness rates on most task categories, with the strongest configurations achieving mean speedups of up to 6.89x on PyTorch-to-HIP, 6.69x on HIP-to-HIP, and 2.13x on Triton-to-Triton tasks. Our unseen-configuration evaluation shows that HIP-to-HIP and Triton-to-Triton optimizations largely transfer to unseen input shapes, while PyTorch-to-HIP exhibits substantial correctness drops, indicating that agents generating kernels from scratch frequently hardcode shape-specific assumptions. AgentKernelArena is designed as a modular, extensible framework for rigorous evaluation of agentic GPU kernel optimization across agents, tasks, and hardware targets.

</details>


### [100] [Constrained Code Generation with Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16829)
*Lize Shao,Michael Cardei,Zichen Xie,Ferdinando Fioretto,Wenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出约束扩散编码（CDC），一种无需训练的神经符号推理框架，通过在离散扩散模型的反向去噪过程中集成约束满足，实现功能正确性、安全性和语法约束的代码生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在代码生成中具有并行令牌细化和全局程序状态暴露的优势，为在最终代码提交前强制执行程序级功能和安全性约束提供了天然干预点，但现有方法未充分利用这一机会。

Method: CDC基于离散扩散采样器，通过约束感知去噪算子增强反向去噪过程，该算子结合数学优化与程序分析，识别中间程序状态中约束相关区域并局部调整去噪轨迹，引导生成走向可行程序。

Result: 在代码生成基准测试中，CDC在功能正确性、安全性和语法约束满足方面持续优于离散扩散和自回归基线，且所需的校正计算更少、编辑更局部化。

Conclusion: 训练自由的神经符号框架CDC能有效将约束满足集成到离散扩散模型的去噪过程中，以更高效的局部编辑提升多维度约束满足能力。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are a powerful, emerging paradigm for code generation. They construct programs through iterative refinement of partially corrupted token sequences and enable parallel token refinement. Importantly, this paradigm exposes a global program state at each denoising step, which provides a natural intervention point for enforcing program-level functionality and security constraints, guiding the generation before the final code is committed. Building on this observation, the paper introduces Constrained Diffusion for Code (CDC), a training-free neurosymbolic inference framework that integrates constraint satisfaction directly into the reverse denoising process. CDC augments the base discrete diffusion sampler with constraint-aware denoising operators that combine mathematical optimization with program analysis to identify constraint-relevant regions of the intermediate program state and locally adjust the denoising trajectory, steering generation toward feasible programs while remaining close to the base model. Across code generation benchmarks, CDC consistently improves constraint satisfaction in functional correctness, security, and even syntax, outperforming discrete diffusion and autoregressive baselines with less corrective computation and more localized edits.

</details>


### [101] [CompactAttention: Accelerating Chunked Prefill with Block-Union KV Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16839)
*Jiwon Song,Dongwon Jo,Beomseok Kang,Jae-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CompactAttention，一种基于Block-Union KV Selection的分块预填充注意力机制，通过在分块预填充场景下将2D块稀疏掩码转换为GQA感知的每组分KV块表，实现无需显式KV压缩的原位访问，在LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct上RULER基准测试中保持接近密集注意力的精度，同时在128K上下文长度下实现高达2.72倍注意力加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块预填充已成为长上下文大语言模型的主流服务策略，但该场景下的高效注意力计算仍具挑战。现有稀疏注意力方法主要针对一次性预填充设计，无法高效迁移到分块预填充：块稀疏核在查询长度受限于块大小时效率下降，细粒度模式搜索在每块累积KV缓存时重复计算成本高昂。QUOKA虽直接针对分块预填充，但依赖查询子采样的token级KV选择，可能遗漏查询特定的KV条目并引入显式KV复制开销。

Method: 提出CompactAttention，基于Block-Union KV Selection的分块预填充注意力机制。将2D块稀疏掩码视为KV选择信号而非直接稀疏核执行计划，通过Q-block并集和组内并集将其转换为GQA感知的每组分KV块表。该构造在分页执行约束下生成保留输入掩码所选所有KV块的最小块表，使选中的KV块能够原位访问而无需显式KV压缩。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct上，RULER基准测试中保持接近密集注意力的精度，同时在128K上下文长度的分块预填充下实现高达2.72倍注意力加速。

Conclusion: CompactAttention通过创新的Block-Union KV Selection机制，有效解决了分块预填充场景下稀疏注意力计算的效率与精度权衡问题，无需显式KV复制开销即可实现高效原位访问。

Abstract: Chunked prefill has become a widely adopted serving strategy for long-context large language models, but efficient attention computation in this regime remains challenging. Existing sparse attention methods are primarily designed for one-shot prefill and do not translate efficiently to chunked prefill: block-sparse kernels lose efficiency when the query length is limited by the chunk size, while fine-grained pattern search becomes costly when repeated over the accumulated KV cache at every chunk. QUOKA, a recent method that directly targets chunked prefill, avoids sparse-kernel overhead but relies on query-subsampled, token-level KV selection, which can miss query-specific KV entries and introduce explicit KV-copy overhead. To address these limitations, we propose CompactAttention, a chunked-prefill attention mechanism based on Block-Union KV Selection. CompactAttention treats 2D block-sparse masks as KV-selection signals rather than direct sparse-kernel execution plans, and converts them into GQA-aware per-group KV block tables through Q-block union and intra-group union. This construction produces the minimal block tables that preserve all KV blocks selected by the input masks under paged execution constraints, enabling selected KV blocks to be accessed in place without explicit KV compaction. On LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct, CompactAttention maintains accuracy close to dense attention on the RULER benchmark while delivering up to 2.72$\times$ attention speedup at 128K context length under chunked prefill.

</details>


### [102] [RTI-Bench: A Structured Dataset for Indian Right-to-Information Decision Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16843)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RTI-Bench，首个针对印度《信息权法》中央信息委员会（CIC）行政裁决的结构化数据集，包含1218个案例（规则提取标注）和298个PDF裁决（五名专员，2023-2026年），标注覆盖率89%（指令-回复语料）和51%（PDF子集），标签精确率95.3%，零样本Mistral 7B在结果预测上达57.3%准确率和37.0%宏F1，远超多数类基线14.3%宏F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度《信息权法》赋予公民向公共机构索取信息的权利，但CIC裁决使用密集的行政语言，普通公民难以理解，也无法预判上诉是否值得提起。现有缺乏面向印度RTI行政裁决的公开结构化数据集，阻碍了相关研究和公众可及性的提升。

Method: 构建RTI-Bench数据集：从公开的指令-回复语料（1218个案例）通过基于规则的提取添加结构化字段；从CIC门户直接收集298个PDF裁决（覆盖五名专员、三种文档格式、2023-2026年时期）。结构化字段包括结果标签、豁免引用、IRAC式推理组件和程序时间线。对50个标注案例进行人工审查评估精确率。

Result: 指令-回复语料标注覆盖率达89%，PDF子集（239个主要裁决）首批发布覆盖率51%。50个标注案例人工审查的标签精确率为95.3%。零样本Mistral 7B在100个案例上结果预测准确率57.3%，宏F1 37.0%，远高于多数类基线宏F1（14.3%）。

Conclusion: RTI-Bench是首个公开的印度RTI行政裁决结构化数据集，展示了良好的标注质量和基准性能，为辅助公民理解CIC裁决、预判上诉结果以及推动相关NLP研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: India's Right to Information Act, 2005 gives every citizen the right to demand information from public authorities, yet in practice most people cannot make sense of the dense administrative language used in Central Information Commission (CIC) decisions, let alone predict whether an appeal is worth filing. This paper introduces RTI-Bench, a structured dataset of CIC decisions with outcome labels, exemption citations, IRAC-style reasoning components, and procedural timelines. To the best of our knowledge it is the first publicly released structured dataset for Indian RTI administrative decisions. The dataset draws from two sources: 1,218 cases from a publicly available instruction-response corpus (with structured fields added through rule-based extraction), and 298 CIC decision PDFs collected directly from the Commission portal, spanning five commissioners and three document format generations from 2023 to 2026. Label coverage reaches 89% on the instruction-response corpus. For the PDF subset of 239 primary decisions, coverage is 51% in this first release. A random sample of 50 labelled cases was manually reviewed, yielding a label precision of 95.3%. A zero-shot Mistral 7B baseline on 100 cases gives 57.3% accuracy and 37.0% macro-F1 on outcome prediction, well above the majority-class baseline of 14.3% macro-F1. RTI-Bench is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/joyboseroy/rti-bench

</details>


### [103] [MixSD: Mixed Contextual Self-Distillation for Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16865)
*Jiarui Liu,Lechen Zhang,Yongjin Yang,Yinghui He,Yingheng Wang,Weihao Xuan,Zhijing Jin,Mona Diab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MixSD方法，通过混合基础模型自身两种条件（观察到事实的专家条件和反映原始先验的朴素条件）的token来构建监督信号，实现与模型分布对齐的知识注入，在记忆-保留权衡上显著优于标准SFT和on-policy自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT在注入新知识时会退化预训练能力（如推理和通用领域性能），原因是微调目标与模型的自回归分布存在偏差，迫使优化器去模仿低概率token序列，从而导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: MixSD是一种无需外部教师的知识注入方法，通过混合基础模型自身的两种条件——观测到注入事实的专家条件（expert conditional）和反映原始先验的朴素条件（naive conditional）——来动态构建监督序列，使监督信号在保留事实学习信号的同时更贴近基础模型的原始分布。

Result: 在多个模型规模、合成语料和知识编辑基准上，MixSD在记忆-保留权衡上始终优于SFT和on-policy自蒸馏基线，能保留高达100%的基础模型保留能力同时保持接近完美的训练准确率，而标准SFT仅保留1%；MixSD产生的监督目标在基础模型下具有更低的NLL，并减少沿Fisher敏感参数方向的有害移动。

Conclusion: 将监督信号与模型原生生成分布对齐是一种简单且有效的知识注入原则，能够有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely used to inject new knowledge into language models, but it often degrades pretrained capabilities such as reasoning and general-domain performance. We argue this forgetting arises because fine-tuning targets from humans or external systems diverge from the model's autoregressive distribution, forcing the optimizer to imitate low-probability token sequences. To address this problem, we propose MixSD, a simple external-teacher-free method for distribution-aligned knowledge injection. Instead of training on fixed targets, MixSD constructs supervision dynamically by mixing tokens from two conditionals of the base model itself: an expert conditional that observes the injected fact in context, and a naive conditional that reflects the model's original prior. The resulting supervision sequences preserve the factual learning signal while remaining substantially closer to the base model's distribution. We evaluate MixSD on two synthetic corpora that we construct to study factual recall and arithmetic function acquisition in a controlled setting, together with established benchmarks for open-domain factual question answering and knowledge editing. Across multiple model scales and settings, MixSD consistently achieves a better memorization-retention trade-off compared to SFT and on-policy self distillation baselines, retaining up to 100% of the base model's held-out capability while maintaining near-perfect training accuracy, whereas standard SFT retains as little as 1%. We further show that MixSD produces substantially lower-NLL supervision targets under the base model and reduces harmful movement along Fisher-sensitive parameter directions. These results suggest that aligning supervision with the model's native generation distribution is a simple and effective principle for knowledge injection that mitigates catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [104] [PaliBench: A Multi-Reference Blueprint for Classical Language Translation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16881)
*Máté Metzger,Nadnapang Phophichit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍PaliBench，一个面向巴利语-英语翻译的多参考基准，以及一套将经典文献的独立学术翻译转化为评估基础设施的可复用方法，并基于10个当代大语言模型进行了评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有翻译基准对经典文本适配性差，通常使用单一参考译文进行比较，而经典文本允许多种忠实译法，存在术语、语域和解释上的差异。因此需要构建多参考翻译基准来更好地评估机器翻译系统。

Method: 基于《经藏》段落，对齐三位比丘（Sujato、Thanissaro、Bodhi）的独立英译本；使用LLM辅助对齐独立分割的译文，经自动验证、段落级质量过滤、公式化重复去重，最后通过多指标对多参考译文进行评估。

Result: 构建了包含1,700个段落、8,389个片段、约345,000个token的基准；对10个大语言模型的评测显示，系统排名存在跨指标一致性，但可靠性和语义异常率差异显著。

Conclusion: PaliBench展示了如何将现有学术翻译转化为经典文本的评估基础设施，而不将任何单一译文视为权威。该方法可推广至其他拥有足够独立参考译文的经典语料库。

Abstract: Digital humanities projects increasingly rely on machine translation and large language models to widen access to classical, religious, and otherwise under-translated textual traditions. Yet standard translation benchmarks are poorly suited to such materials: they typically compare a system output against a single reference translation, even though classical texts often support multiple faithful renderings that differ in terminology, register, and interpretation. This article introduces PaliBench, both a benchmark for Pali-to-English translation and a reusable method for constructing multi-reference translation benchmarks for classical languages. The Pali case study draws on passages from the Sutta Pitaka aligned with independent English translations by Bhikkhu Sujato, Bhikkhu Thanissaro, and Bhikkhu Bodhi. The workflow combines LLM-assisted alignment of independently segmented translations, automated verification against source files, passage-level quality filtering, deduplication of formulaic repetitions, and multi-metric evaluation against multiple human references. The resulting benchmark contains 1,700 passages spanning 8,389 segments and approximately 345,000 tokens. We use it to evaluate ten contemporary large language models with complementary metrics, finding strong cross-metric concordance in system rankings alongside substantial variation in reliability and semantic outlier rates. The broader contribution is methodological: PaliBench shows how existing scholarly translations can be transformed into evaluation infrastructure for interpretive textual traditions without treating any single translation as definitive. Although developed for Pali Buddhist texts, the approach could be portable to other classical corpora where sufficient independent reference translations exist.

</details>


### [105] [E-PMQ: Expert-Guided Post-Merge Quantization with Merged-Weight Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16882)
*Wenjun Wang,Yanggan Gu,Shuo Cai,Yuanyi Wang,Pengkai Wang,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出E-PMQ框架，解决模型合并后直接量化导致的双重偏差问题，通过专家引导的校准和合并权重锚定，显著提升低比特部署性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化与模型合并是低资源部署的两项关键技术，但直接将后训练量化(PTQ)应用于合并后的模型会耦合两种偏差——低比特重构引入的量化偏差和模型合并继承的专家相对合并偏差，导致性能严重下降。现有方法未针对这一耦合问题进行专门处理。

Method: 提出E-PMQ(Expert-guided Post-Merge Quantization)框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 专家引导的输出目标校准，利用源专家权重在逐层校准过程中提供专家引导的输出目标；2) 合并权重锚定，稳定校准过程并保留合并模型的集成行为。

Result: 在CLIP-ViT-B/32八任务合并中，E-PMQ将4-bit GPTQ从65.0%提升至73.6%(Task Arithmetic)，从69.1%提升至74.8%(TIES-Merging)。在更困难设置下，E-PMQ将20任务CLIP-ViT-L/14的GPTQ从34.8%提升至76.7%，将FLAN-T5-base GLUE从78.26%提升至83.34%。

Conclusion: E-PMQ能够有效实现后合并量化(post-merge quantization)和低比特部署，为低资源场景下整合多专家模型提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Low-resource deployment constraints have made model quantization essential for deploying neural networks while preserving performance. Meanwhile, model merging has become an increasingly practical low-resource strategy for integrating multiple task- or domain-specialized experts into a single model without joint training or multi-model serving. Together, quantization and model merging enable an efficient low-resource deployment pipeline by integrating multiple experts into one low-bit model. We formulate this setting as Post-Merge Quantization (PMQ). We show that directly applying post-training quantization (PTQ) to a merged model is unreliable because two distinct deviations are coupled: the quantization deviation introduced by low-bit reconstruction and the expert-relative merging deviation inherited from model merging. To mitigate these deviations, we propose E-PMQ, an expert-guided PMQ framework that uses source expert weights to provide expert- guided output targets during layer-wise calibration, together with merged-weight anchoring to stabilize the calibration and preserve the integrated behavior of the merged model. On CLIP-ViT-B/32 eight-task merging, E-PMQ improves 4-bit GPTQ from 65.0% to 73.6% under Task Arithmetic and from 69.1% to 74.8% under TIES-Merging. On harder settings, E-PMQ improves GPTQ from 34.8% to 76.7% on 20-task CLIP-ViT-L/14 and from 78.26% to 83.34% on FLAN-T5- base GLUE. These results demonstrate that E-PMQ enables effective post-merge quantization and low-bit deployment.

</details>


### [106] [Full Attention Strikes Back: Transferring Full Attention into Sparse within Hundred Training Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16928)
*Yanke Zhou,Yiduo Li,Hanlin Tang,Maohua Li,Kan Liu,Lan Tao,Lin Qu,Yuan Yao,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RTPurbo，利用全注意力LLM内在的稀疏性，通过识别少数需要全上下文的检索头、使用16维索引器高效检索相关token以及动态top-p选择，仅需少量训练步骤即可实现高效稀疏推理，在长上下文任务中实现近无损精度和显著加速（1M上下文预填充加速9.36倍，解码加速2.01倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全注意力机制在处理长上下文时面临二次成本的瓶颈，现有替代方案（原生稀疏训练或启发式token淘汰）在效率、训练成本和准确性之间存在不可取的权衡。作者希望证明标准全注意力LLM本身已具有内在稀疏性，可以通过少量适配转化为高度稀疏模型，从而避免昂贵的原生稀疏预训练。

Method: 基于三个观察：（1）仅少数注意力头真正需要全上下文处理；（2）长程检索主要由低维子空间控制，可用16维索引器高效检索相关token；（3）有用token预算高度依赖查询，动态top-p选择优于固定top-k。RTPurbo仅对检索头保留完整KV缓存，引入轻量级token索引器实现稀疏注意力，仅需数百训练步骤即可完成稀疏化。

Result: 在长上下文基准和推理任务上，RTPurbo实现了近无损精度，同时提供显著效率提升：1M上下文时预填充加速达9.36倍，解码加速约2.01倍。

Conclusion: 标准全注意力训练可以获得强稀疏推理能力，无需昂贵的原生稀疏预训练，打破了精度与效率之间的传统权衡。

Abstract: Long-context inference in large language models is bottlenecked by the quadratic cost of full attention. Existing efficient alternatives often rely either on native sparse training or on heuristic token eviction, creating an undesirable trade-off among efficiency, training cost, and accuracy. In this work, we show that full-attention LLMs are already intrinsically sparse and can be transformed into highly sparse models with only minimal adaptation. Our approach is built on three observations: (1) only a small subset of attention heads truly requires full long-context processing; (2) long-range retrieval is governed primarily by a low-dimensional subspace, allowing relevant tokens to be retrieved efficiently with a 16-dimensional indexer; and (3) the useful token budget is strongly query-dependent, making dynamic top-$p$ selection more suitable than fixed top-$k$ sparsification. Based on these insights, we propose RTPurbo, which retains the full KV cache only for retrieval heads and introduces a lightweight token indexer for sparse attention. By exploiting the model's intrinsic sparsity, RTPurbo achieves sparsification with only a few hundred training steps. Experiments on long-context benchmarks and reasoning tasks show that RTPurbo preserves near-lossless accuracy while delivering substantial efficiency gains, including up to a 9.36$\times$ prefill speedup at 1M context and about a 2.01$\times$ decode speedup. These results suggest that strong sparse inference can be obtained from standard full-attention training without expensive native sparse pretraining.

</details>


### [107] [Effort as Ceiling, Not Dial: Reasoning Budget Does Not Modulate Cognitive Cost Alignment Between Humans and Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16938)
*Yueqing Hu,Tianhong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究大型推理模型(LRM)的链式思维追踪长度与人类反应时间之间的对齐是否随推理时努力程度变化。实验发现，无论任务内还是跨任务，这种对齐在三个努力水平上均保持不变，表明对齐是训练时形成的编译特性，而非推理时的在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究显示LRM的链式思维长度与人类认知反应时间高度对齐，但存在争议：这种对齐反映的是真实的计算结构耦合，还是仅仅是表面的冗长性？作者希望通过操控推理时的计算努力程度来区分这两种解释。

Method: 使用GPT-OSS-20B和GPT-OSS-120B两个模型，设置三个推理努力水平，在六个推理任务上测量链式思维token长度与人类反应时间的对齐程度。通过贝叶斯因子检验零假设，对比算术复杂度中token分配与人类难度模式，并设置操控检验验证努力参数的实际效果。

Result: (1) 对齐程度在不同努力水平下数值上几乎相同，贝叶斯因子支持零假设；(2) 操控检验发现"努力"参数实际设置的是生成的上限预算，而非驱动实时资源分配；(3) 算术复杂度对比显示token分配追踪了细粒度的、格式依赖的人类难度模式，且更大模型匹配度更高。

Conclusion: LRM与人类之间的认知成本对齐是训练时实现的成果，对推理时的扰动具有鲁棒性。这支持LRM问题解决能力是一种"编译型"(compiled)而非"在线型"(online)计算过程的观点。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought traces whose length tracks human reaction times across cognitive tasks, but recent debate questions whether this alignment reflects genuine computational structure or surface verbosity. We test whether the alignment varies with inference-time reasoning effort. Across GPT-OSS-20B and GPT-OSS-120B, three effort levels, and six reasoning tasks, within-task and cross-task alignment remain invariant: Bayes Factors lean toward the null, and mean alignment is numerically near-identical across conditions. A manipulation check reveals that the effort parameter sets an upper budget on generation rather than driving real-time allocation, suggesting that the allocation policy is crystallized at training time. Arithmetic complexity contrasts further show that token allocation tracks fine-grained, format-dependent human difficulty patterns, with model scale improving the match. Cognitive cost alignment between LRMs and humans appears to be a training-time achievement, robust to inference-time perturbations, supporting a compiled rather than online account of LRM problem-solving.

</details>


### [108] [Closing the Gap at CRAC 2026: Two-Stage Adaptation for LLM-Based Multilingual Coreference Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16984)
*Antoine Bourgois,Olga Seminck,Thierry Poibeau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Gemma-3-27b的两阶段微调策略（多语言基座适配器+数据集专用适配器）的指代消解系统，采用基于头词的XML格式表示提及跨度、局部重索引和迭代标注方法，在CRAC 2026 LLM赛道中以74.32 CoNLL F1成绩获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与CRAC 2026共享任务中的LLM赛道，旨在利用大语言模型提升指代消解性能，尤其是在多语言、多文档长度和不同标注规范下的泛化能力。

Method: 基于Gemma-3-27b模型，采用两阶段微调策略：第一阶段训练多语言基座适配器，第二阶段训练数据集专用适配器。提及跨度表示采用基于头词的XML格式并引入局部重索引，文档采用迭代式标注方法。
