On 8K frequent ICD-10 codes, the multi-version training also substantially improves macro metrics, with far fewer model parameters.

</details>


### [145] [Internalizing Tool Knowledge in Small Language Models via QLoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17774)
*Yuval Shemla,Ayal Yakobe,Tanmay Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过参数高效微调（QLoRA）将工具使用知识内化到小型语言模型中，使其在推理时无需完整工具描述即可进行结构化规划。在AssetOpsBench上，微调后的Gemma 4 E4B和Qwen3-4B模型在减少82.6%输入长度的同时，规划性能超过包含完整工具描述的非微调基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用的流水线需要在每个提示中包含完整的工具模式，造成大量token开销，限制了小型模型的实用性。本文旨在探索能否通过参数高效微调，将工具使用知识内化到小模型权重中，从而在推理时无需显式工具描述即可进行结构化规划。

Method: 使用AssetOpsBench作为主要基准，采用8位QLoRA对Gemma 4 E4B和Qwen3-4B进行微调，训练数据包含约1,700个工具使用样本，涵盖工具知识、问题到规划的映射以及执行风格轨迹。在推理时采用无描述推理模式，提示中完全省略工具目录。

Result: 微调模型在减少82.6%输入长度的同时，规划分数优于包含完整工具描述的非微调基线。Gemma最佳运行获得AT-F1为0.65、整体judge分数3.88（基线为0.47和2.88）。Qwen3-4B获得整体judge分数3.78，内存使用减少62%，速度提升2.5倍，但在通用多项选择基准上表现出更大的灾难性遗忘。LoRA秩控制质量与保留的权衡，r=32最大化规划质量，较小秩保留更多通用知识。

Conclusion: 对于固定工具目录，QLoRA微调可以将工具知识从提示上下文转移到模型权重中，大幅降低推理开销的同时保持或提升工具规划质量。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as planning components in agentic systems, but current tool-use pipelines often require full tool schemas to be included in every prompt, creating substantial token overhead and limiting the practicality of smaller models. This paper investigates whether tool-use knowledge can be internalized into small language models through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, enabling structured planning without explicit tool descriptions at inference time. Using AssetOpsBench as the primary benchmark, we fine-tune Gemma 4 E4B and Qwen3-4B with 8-bit QLoRA on approximately 1,700 tool-use examples spanning tool knowledge, question-to-plan mappings, and execution-style traces. We evaluate the resulting models under description-free inference, where the prompt omits the tool catalog entirely. The fine-tuned models outperform an informed unfine-tuned baseline that receives full tool descriptions, reducing input length by 82.6\% while improving structural and LLM-judge planning scores. In the best Gemma run, the model achieves an AT-F1 of 0.65 and an overall judge score of 3.88, compared with 0.47 and 2.88 for the informed baseline. Qwen3-4B achieves a strong overall judge score of 3.78 while using 62\% less memory and running 2.5$\times$ faster than Gemma, though it also exhibits greater catastrophic forgetting on general multiple-choice benchmarks. Additional ablations show that LoRA rank controls a quality--retention trade-off, with $r=32$ maximizing planning quality and smaller ranks preserving more general knowledge. These results suggest that, for fixed tool catalogs, QLoRA fine-tuning can shift tool knowledge from prompt context into model weights, substantially reducing inference overhead while maintaining or improving tool-planning quality.

</details>


### [146] [Systematic Evaluation of the Quality of Synthetic Clinical Notes Rephrased by LLMs at Million-Note Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17775)
*Jinghui Liu,Sarvesh Soni,Anthony Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM生成的临床文本，在百万级MIMIC笔记上进行了内在、外在和事实性评估。研究发现合成笔记保留了粗粒度任务的临床信息和预测效用，但丢失了细粒度细节（如ICD编码）；分块改写可减轻细节丢失但会降低事实精度；错误主要源于临床语境误解；合成笔记可有效增强罕见ICD代码的任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM生成的临床文本评估往往只关注单一维度（如相似性或效用），而忽略了这些方面的互补性。作者旨在进行全面系统的评估，涵盖内在、外在和事实性三个维度。

Method: 使用LLM从MIMIC数据库以百万级规模重写临床笔记，进行三重评估：1) 内在评估（语言变化分析）；2) 外在评估（粗粒度任务预测效用、细粒度ICD编码任务）；3) 事实性评估（事实验证与错误分析）。比较全笔记改写与分块改写两种策略。

Result: 合成笔记保留了核心临床信息和粗粒度任务的预测效用，但丢失了ICD编码所需的细粒度细节；分块改写可缓解细节丢失但降低事实精度；错误以临床语境误解为主；合成笔记可有效增强罕见ICD编码的训练数据。

Conclusion: LLM生成的临床文本在不同评估维度上表现差异显著，需根据下游任务需求选择改写策略，分块改写与全笔记改写之间存在精度与细节的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate or synthesize clinical text for a wide range of applications, from improving clinical documentation to augmenting clinical text analytics. Yet evaluations typically focus on a narrow aspect -- such as similarity or utility comparisons -- even though these aspects are complementary and best viewed in parallel. In this study, we aim to conduct a systematic evaluation of LLM-generated clinical text, which includes intrinsic, extrinsic, and factuality evaluations of synthetic clinical notes rephrased from MIMIC databases at million-note scale. Our analysis demonstrates that synthetic notes preserve core clinical information and predictive utility for coarse-grained tasks despite substantial linguistic changes, but lose fine-grained details for task like ICD coding. We show this loss of detail can be substantially mitigated by rephrasing notes by chunks rather than by the whole note, but at the cost of reduced factual precision under incomplete context. Through fact-checking and error analysis, we further find that synthesis errors are dominated by misinterpretation of clinical context, alongside temporal confusion, measurement errors, and fabricated claims. Finally, we show that the synthetic notes -- despite their task-agnostic nature -- can effectively augment task-specific training for rare ICD codes.

</details>


### [147] [Generating Pretraining Tokens from Organic Data for Data-Bound Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17849)
*Zichun Yu,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynPro是一个面向数据受限场景的合成数据生成框架，通过改写和重格式化两种操作，利用强化学习优化生成器，使同一份有机数据呈现多样化形式，帮助LLM更充分地学习有限数据。实验表明，在400M和1.1B规模上，SynPro能解锁3.7-5.2倍于标准重复的有效token量，甚至超越非数据受限的Oracle基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练正从计算受限转向数据受限，可用的人类(有机)文本远低于规模化需求。然而，进入数据受限阶段并不意味着模型已充分利用其有机语料库，现有方法对数据的利用效率不足。

Method: SynPro框架包含两个核心操作：改写(rephrasing)和重格式化(reformat)，将同一份有机源数据呈现为多样化形式。两个生成器通过强化学习进行优化，奖励信号包括质量、忠实度和数据影响力三方面。生成器会在预训练出现平台期时持续更新，针对模型尚未吸收的内容生成数据。

Result: 在400M和1.1B参数规模上，使用Chinchilla最优token数的10%（0.8B和2.2B）进行预训练，SynPro相比标准重复能解锁3.7-5.2倍的有效token量，在1.1B规模上甚至超过了使用等量唯一数据的非数据受限Oracle基线。分析证实，忠实且模型感知的合成能够维持数据受限下的规模化扩展，不会导致分布坍塌。

Conclusion: 有机数据在标准重复策略下被严重低效利用，SynPro通过模型感知、持续更新的合成数据生成策略，能够显著提升有限有机数据的利用效率，为数据受限条件下的LLM预训练提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: LLM pretraining is shifting from a compute-bound to a data-bound regime, where available human (organic) text falls far short of scaling demands. However, reaching the data-bound regime does not mean the model has fully utilized its organic corpus. In this paper, we introduce SynPro, a synthetic data generation framework that helps LLMs more thoroughly learn from limited organic data. SynPro applies two operations, rephrasing and reformat, that present the same organic source in diverse forms to facilitate deeper learning without introducing external information. Both generators are optimized via reinforcement learning with quality, faithfulness, and data influence rewards, and are continuously updated as pretraining plateaus to target content the model has yet to absorb. We pretrain 400M and 1.1B models with 10% of their Chinchilla-optimal tokens (0.8B and 2.2B) from DCLM-Baseline, reflecting a realistic data-bound regime in frontier pretraining. Our results reveal that organic data is significantly underutilized by standard repetition: SynPro unlocks 3.7-5.2x the effective tokens of repetition, even surpassing the non-data-bound oracle that trains on equivalent unique data at the 1.1B scale. Analyses confirm that faithful, model-aware synthesis sustains data-bound scaling without causing distribution collapse. We open-source our code at https://github.com/cxcscmu/SynPro.

</details>


### [148] [PAREDA: A Multi-Accent Speech Dataset of Natural Language Processing Research Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17860)
*Sicheng Jin,Dipankar Srirag,Aditya Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍PAREDA数据集——首个包含澳大利亚、印度英语和中文英语口音的学术论文讨论多口音语音数据集，涵盖自发独白和非独白对话。实验表明，SOTA ASR模型在零样本设置下表现较差，但微调后词错误率显著下降，证明该数据集能捕捉现有语料库缺乏的语言特征，有助于构建更鲁棒和包容的ASR系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统在基准语料库上表现优异，但在真实场景中因口音、自发性和领域特异性语音变异性导致性能下降，缺乏针对学术领域多口音语音的数据集。

Method: 构建PAREDA数据集，包含澳大利亚、印度英语和中文英语口音 speakers 对NLP论文的讨论，包括自发独白（摘要总结）和非独白（问答环节）。使用SOTA ASR模型进行零样本评估和微调实验，分析口音混合和语速增加的影响。

Result: 零样本设置下模型表现较差，验证了数据集的高挑战性；在PAREDA上微调后词错误率（WER）显著降低，证明数据集有效捕捉了现有语料库缺失的语言特征。

Conclusion: PAREDA为构建和评估针对专业化、真实世界应用的更鲁棒和包容的ASR系统提供了有价值的资源。

Abstract: While modern Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems achieve high accuracy on benchmark corpora, their performance often degrades when there is real-world variability. This work focuses on variability arising due to accented, spontaneous, and domain-specific speech. In particular, we introduce PAper REading DAtaset (PAREDA), a first-of-its-kind multi-accent speech dataset consisting of discussions on academic Natural Language Processing (NLP) papers between speakers with Australian, Indian-English, and Chinese English accents. Each session elicits a spontaneous monologue (a summary of a paper's abstract) and a non-monologue (a question-and-answer session between participants), resulting in a corpus rich with technical jargon and conversational phenomena. We evaluate the performance of SOTA ASR models on PAREDA, analysing the impact of accent mixing and increased speech rate. Our results show that, in the zero-shot setting, models perform worse, confirming the dataset's challenging nature. However, fine-tuning on PAREDA significantly reduces the Word Error Rate (WER), demonstrating that our dataset captures linguistic characteristics often missing from existing corpora. PAREDA serves as a valuable new resource for building and evaluating more robust and inclusive ASR systems for specialised, real-world applications.

</details>


### [149] [Multi-agent AI systems outperform human teams in creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17885)
*Tiancheng Hu,Yixuan Jiang,Haotian Li,José Hernández-Orallo,Xing Xie,Nigel Collier,David Stillwell,Luning Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM团队在六项创造性任务中不仅超越单智能体，还显著优于人类团队（Cohen's d=1.50），优势源于新颖性提升而实用性相当。通过语义空间路径分析发现，LLM与人类团队的高创造力均与低全局连贯性相关，但预测创造力的其他模式不同：LLM团队受益于高效探索（高语义扩散、短路径），人类团队受益于流畅对话（高局部连贯性、频繁转折）。模型选择与讨论结构可解释26.8%的对话动态方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的AI系统在研究和创新中日益普及，理解和增强AI的创造力已成为关键挑战。创造力被视为人类智能的竞争前沿，亟需系统探究多智能体LLM系统能否以及如何超越人类团队的创造性表现。

Method: 研究对比了多智能体LLM团队（4,541个创意）与人类团队（341个创意）在六项多样性问题解决任务上的创造力表现。使用神经语言模型表征将对话表示为语义空间中的路径，分析全局连贯性、语义扩散、路径长度、局部连贯性和转折频率等对话动态指标。

Result: 多智能体LLM团队显著超越人类团队（Cohen's d=1.50），优势主要由新颖性驱动，实用性相当。两类团队在低全局连贯性时创造力均更高，但差异在于：LLM团队高效探索（高语义扩散、短路径）更有利，人类团队则需流畅对话（高局部连贯性、频繁转折）。模型选择和讨论结构是正交设计杠杆，共同解释26.8%的对话动态差异。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统在创造力任务上可超越人类团队，其对话动态可通过系统化设计进行调控，为开发增强创造力的人机协作系统提供了理论依据和工程路径。

Abstract: Although artificial intelligence (AI) now matches or exceeds human performance across numerous cognitive tasks, creativity remains a highly contested frontier. As AI systems based on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted in research and innovation, it is essential to understand and augment their creativity. Here we demonstrate that multi-agent LLM teams not only surpass single agents, but also substantially outperform human teams in creativity (Cohen's d=1.50) across 4,541 multi-agent LLM ideas and 341 human-team ideas on six diverse problem-solving tasks. This advantage is driven by novelty while maintaining comparable usefulness. To investigate the generative processes in both groups, we represent conversations as paths through semantic space using neural language model representations. Both LLM and human teams produce more creative ideas when conversations range widely rather than staying centered on a single theme (low global coherence). However, the additional patterns that predict creativity differ: LLM teams benefit from efficient exploration (high semantic spread, shorter paths), while human teams benefit from maintaining smooth conversational flow (high local coherence, frequent pivots). Additionally, we identify model choice and discussion structure as orthogonal design levers that together explain 26.8% of variance in LLM conversational dynamics, paving the way for systematic approaches to developing multi-agent systems with augmented creative capabilities.

</details>


### [150] [Universal Adversarial Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17936)
*Benedict Florance Arockiaraj,Alexander Feng,Jianxiong Cai,Xiaoyu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出结合词性过滤（POS filtering）和基于困惑度的损失函数（perplexity-based loss）生成更自然、更符合语法的通用对抗触发器（universal adversarial triggers），在SST情感分析任务上将准确率降至0.04（正向转负向）和0.12（负向转正向），并通过对抗训练将模型鲁棒性从0.12提升至0.48。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用对抗攻击生成的触发器不语法、不自然，容易被检测，因此需要生成更接近自然短语的触发器，使对抗攻击更难被察觉。

Method: 1) 结合词性过滤（POS filtering）约束触发器的词性组成；2) 引入基于困惑度（perplexity）的损失函数使触发器在语言上更流畅自然；3) 利用生成的触发器进行对抗训练以提升模型鲁棒性。

Result: 在SST数据集上，正向转负向攻击准确率降至0.04，负向转正向攻击准确率降至0.12；对抗训练后模型准确率从0.12提升至0.48。

Conclusion: 通过生成更自然的对抗触发器可以使攻击更难被检测，同时对抗训练能有效提升模型对这类攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent works have illustrated that modern NLP models trained for diverse tasks ranging from sentiment analysis to language generation succumb to universal adversarial attacks, a class of input-agnostic attacks where a common trigger sequence is used to attack the model. Although these attacks are successful, the triggers generated by such attacks are ungrammatical and unnatural. Our work proposes a novel technique combining parts-of-speech filtering and perplexity based loss function to generate sensible triggers that are closer to natural phrases. For the task of sentiment analysis on the SST dataset, the method produces sensible triggers that achieve accuracies as low as 0.04 and 0.12 for flipping positive to negative predictions and vice-versa. To build robust models, we also perform adversarial training using the generated triggers that increases the accuracy of the model from 0.12 to 0.48. We aim to illustrate that adversarial attacks can be made difficult to detect by generating sensible triggers, and to facilitate robust model development through relevant defenses.

</details>


### [151] [BacktestBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Automated Quantitative Strategy Backtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17937)
*Zhensheng Wang,Wenmian Yang,Qingtai Wu,Lequan Ma,Yiquan Zhang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BacktestBench——首个大规模自动化量化回测基准，包含18,246个标注问答对，以及AutoBacktest——一个多智能体基线系统，用于将自然语言策略转化为可复现的回测。在23个主流LLM上的评估识别了影响端到端性能的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化回测是评估交易策略的核心，但存在高技术门槛和可扩展性差的问题。LLM为自动化这一复杂流程提供了可能，然而缺乏大规模基准数据集严重阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了基于600万条真实市场记录的BacktestBench基准（含18,246个问答对，覆盖指标计算、标的选择、策略选择、参数确认四类任务）；提出了AutoBacktest多智能体基线方法，协调Summarizer（语义因子提取）、Retriever（SQL生成）、Coder（Python回测实现）三个智能体。

Result: 在23个主流LLM上进行了评估和消融实验，识别了影响端到端性能的关键因素，强调了grounded verification和标准化指标表示的重要性。

Conclusion: BacktestBench和AutoBacktest为自动化量化回测提供了标准化评估框架和有效基线，揭示了当前LLM在该任务中的能力边界和改进方向。

Abstract: Quantitative backtesting is essential for evaluating trading strategies but remains hampered by high technical barriers and limited scalability. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a transformative path to automate this complex, interdisciplinary workflow through advanced code generation, tool usage, and agentic planning, the practical realization is significantly challenged by the current lack of a large-scale benchmark dedicated to automated quantitative backtesting, which hinders progress in this field. To bridge this critical gap, we introduce BacktestBench, the first large-scale benchmark for automated quantitative backtesting. Built from over 6 million real market records, it comprises 18,246 meticulously annotated question-answering pairs across four task categories: metrics calculation, ticker selection, strategy selection, and parameter confirmation. We also propose AutoBacktest, a robust multi-agent baseline that translates natural language strategies into reproducible backtests by coordinating a Summarizer for semantic factor extraction, a Retriever for validated SQL generation, and a Coder for Python backtesting implementation. Our evaluation on 23 mainstream LLMs, complemented by targeted ablations, identifies key factors that influence end-to-end performance and highlights the importance of grounded verification and standardized indicator representations.

</details>


### [152] [AutoVecCoder: Teaching LLMs to Generate Explicitly Vectorized Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17978)
*Shangzhan Li,Xinyu Yin,Xuanyu Jin,Ye He,Yuxin Zhou,Yuxuan Li,Xu Han,Wanxiang Che,Qi Shi,Ting Liu,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoVecCoder是一个结合自动化数据合成管道VecPrompt和强化学习框架VecRL的框架，旨在让大语言模型具备自动显式向量化能力。AutoVecCoder-8B在SSE和AVX子集上达到最先进性能，部分情况下超越-O3优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译器自动向量化因保守的静态分析常产生次优结果，开发者被迫使用intrinsics进行显式向量化；而LLM由于缺乏高质量语料和受限于底层硬件指令的严格语义约束，在显式向量化任务上表现不佳。作者旨在让LLM具备自动显式向量化的能力，以克服传统自动向量化的固有瓶颈。

Method: 提出AutoVecCoder框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) VecPrompt——自动化数据合成管道，用于注入领域特定的intrinsic知识；(2) VecRL——基于强化学习的对齐框架，将代码生成与执行效率对齐。基于该框架训练得到AutoVecCoder-8B模型。

Result: AutoVecCoder-8B在SimdBench的SSE和AVX子集上达到最先进性能，部分情况下生成的实现超越了标准-O3优化。

Conclusion: AutoVecCoder框架有效克服了传统自动向量化的固有瓶颈，证明了结合自动化数据合成与强化学习可以使LLM在显式向量化这一低层硬件优化任务上取得突破性进展。

Abstract: Vectorization via Single Instruction, Multiple Data (SIMD) architectures is a cornerstone of high-performance computing. To fully exploit hardware potential, developers often resort to explicit vectorization using intrinsics, as compiler-based auto-vectorization frequently yields suboptimal results due to conservative static analysis. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in general code generation, they struggle with explicit vectorization due to the scarcity of high-quality corpora and the strict semantic constraints of low-level hardware instructions. In this paper, we propose AutoVecCoder, a novel framework designed to empower LLMs with the capability of automated explicit vectorization. AutoVecCoder integrates two core components: VecPrompt, an automated data synthesis pipeline to inject domain-specific intrinsic knowledge; and VecRL, a reinforcement learning framework that aligns code generation with execution efficiency. AutoVecCoder-8B trained by this framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on the SSE and AVX subsets of SimdBench and, in some cases, generates implementations surpassing standard -O3 optimizations, effectively overcoming the inherent bottlenecks of traditional automated vectorization.

</details>


### [153] [Predictive Prefetching for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17989)
*Wuyang Zhang,Shichao Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种先进的异步检索增强生成框架，通过预测性预取与语义前兆检测，实现检索与生成并行化，在保持答案质量的同时显著降低端到端延迟和首令牌时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG系统在检索时采用同步方式，导致显著延迟。虽然已有异步检索工作，但依赖启发式协调，且假设解码过程中信息需求稳定，在多领域复杂场景下往往失败。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个组件的异步检索框架：检索预测器（预测何时触发检索）、上下文监控器（监控生成动态）和查询生成器（生成检索查询）。通过利用生成动力学中的语义前兆，在不确定性达到临界点之前进行预测性预取。

Result: 在多个基准测试上实现了高达43.5%的端到端延迟降低和62.4%的首令牌时间改进，同时答案质量与同步RAG基线相当。

Conclusion: 通过预测性预取和对演化信息需求的动态对齐，所提出的框架能够在不牺牲答案质量的前提下，大幅提升RAG系统的响应速度，解决了异步RAG中协调机制不灵活的问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves factual grounding in large language models but suffers from substantial latency due to synchronous retrieval. While recent work explores asynchronous retrieval, existing approaches rely on heuristic coordination between retrieval and generation and assume stable information demands during decoding that often break in complex, multi-domain settings. In this paper, we propose an advanced asynchronous retrieval framework that enables predictive prefetching aligned with evolving information needs. The framework explicitly predicts when retrieval should be triggered and what information should be retrieved using three components, a retrieval predictor, a context monitor, and a query generator, by exploiting semantic precursors in generation dynamics that emerge several tokens before uncertainty becomes critical. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate up to 43.5% end-to-end latency reduction and 62.4% improvement in time-to-first-token, while maintaining answer quality comparable to synchronous RAG baselines.

</details>


### [154] [KVDrive: A Holistic Multi-Tier KV Cache Management System for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18071)
*Jian Lin,Jiazhi Mi,Zicong Hong,Haodong Wang,Qianli Liu,Haodyue Zhang,Peng Li,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KVDrive是一个面向长上下文LLM的多层级KV缓存管理系统，通过联合优化GPU内存、主机DRAM和SSD间的缓存放置、流水线调度和跨层协调，在严格GPU预算下实现高吞吐推理，相比现有工作吞吐量提升达1.74倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存卸载策略通过将完整缓存存储在主机内存中并选择性取回关键条目，但稀疏化已遇到精度瓶颈，无法进一步压缩；随着上下文长度和批次规模增长，KV传输量急剧增加，成为解码延迟的主要来源。

Method: KVDrive采用系统性方案：1) 根据注意力行为自适应管理缓存以最大化重用并减少冗余数据传输；2) 重构解码流水线，使I/O与CPU/GPU计算阶段重叠，消除异构资源间的停顿；3) 协调跨内存层级的数据移动，突破GPU和DRAM限制实现可扩展的长上下文推理。

Result: 在长上下文基准测试和主流LLM上的评估表明，KVDrive在保持精度的前提下，相比现有最优工作实现高达1.74倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: KVDrive证明了从系统角度而非仅从算法角度解决KV缓存管理问题的有效性，通过多层级协同优化能够显著提升长上下文LLM推理的吞吐性能。

Abstract: Supporting long-context LLMs is challenging due to the substantial memory demands of the key-value (KV) cache. Existing offloading systems store the full cache in host memory and selectively fetch critical entries during decoding, but this strategy quickly hits a ceiling: sparsity cannot be pushed further without degrading accuracy. As a result, when context length and batch size grow, the volume of KV transfers rises sharply and becomes the dominant source of decoding latency. We present KVDrive, a holistic multi-tier KV cache management system spanning GPU memory, host DRAM, and SSD. Unlike prior work that pursues greater sparsity through algorithmic refinements, KVDrive tackles the problem from a systems perspective - jointly orchestrating cache placement, pipeline scheduling, and cross-tier coordination to sustain high-throughput inference under tight GPU budgets. KVDrive advances three fundamental capabilities: it adapts cache management to attention behavior to maximize reuse and minimize redundant data movement; it restructures the decoding pipeline to overlap I/O- and CPU/GPU compute-bound stages, eliminating stalls across heterogeneous resources; and it harmonizes data movement across memory tiers to unlock scalable long-context inference far beyond GPU and DRAM limits. We have implemented a fully functional prototype of KVDrive and evaluated it on long-context benchmarks with popular LLMs. The system achieves up to 1.74x higher throughput compared to state-of-the-art works while preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [155] [A Data-Efficient Path to Multilingual LLMs: Language Expansion via Post-training PARAM$Δ$ Integration into Upcycled MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18083)
*Hao Zhou,Tianhao Li,Zhijun Wang,Shuaijie She,Linjuan Wu,Hao-Ran Wei,Baosong Yang,Jiajun Chen,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为\method的方法，通过将密集模型升级为混合专家（MoE）架构，为不同语言分配不同专家，并通过嫁接MoE扩展的参数增量（Δ_post）到CPT增强的基础模型，解决了多语言扩展中的参数冲突问题，无需复杂对齐即可提升新语言性能并保留原有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型扩展到新语言成本高昂，需要持续的继续预训练和数据密集型对齐。现有的数据免融合技术试图通过融合多语言CPT增强模型与指令版本绕过对齐，但存在关键权衡：缓解参数冲突以保留原始能力不可避免地会削弱新语言习得，反之亦然。本文旨在解决这一冲突。

Method: 提出\method方法，包含两个核心步骤：（1）将密集模型升级为混合专家（MoE）架构，为不同语言分配不同的专家；（2）将MoE扩展的参数增量（Δ_post）嫁接到CPT增强的基础模型上，从而绕过复杂的对齐阶段。

Result: 实验表明，\method方法优于具有类似FLOPs或参数数量的基线方法；它在提升扩展语言性能的同时，有效保留了原始能力。此外，该方法在不同模型和不同后训练增量上均具有高度的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了多语言LLM扩展中保留原始能力与获取新语言能力之间的冲突，通过MoE架构实现语言特定的专家分配，并提出参数增量嫁接策略，在无需复杂对齐的情况下实现了性能提升，具有较好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Expanding Large Language Models~(LLMs) to new languages is a costly endeavor, demanding extensive Continued Pre-Training~(CPT) and data-intensive alignment. While recent data-free merging techniques attempt to bypass alignment by fusing a multilingual CPT-enhanced model with its instruct counterpart, they are plagued by a critical trade-off: mitigating parameter conflicts to preserve original abilities inevitably dilutes new language acquisition, and vice-versa. To resolve this conflict, we introduce \method, which upcycles a dense model into a Mixture-of-Experts~(MoE) architecture, allocating different experts to different languages. Alignment ability is then transferred by grafting a MoE-expanded parameter delta~($Δ_{\text{post}}$) to the CPT-enhanced base model, bypassing the complex alignment phase. Experiments demonstrate \method's superiority even against baselines with similar FLOPs or number of parameters; it improves performance on expanded languages while effectively preserving original capabilities. We further show our approach is highly applicable across different models and Post-training deltas.

</details>


### [156] [How Loud Rumbles Hit Newsstands: A Data Analysis of Coverage and Spatial Bias in German News about Landslides Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18105)
*Brielen Madureira,Andreas Niekler,Marc Keuschnigg,Mariana Madruga de Brito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了25年间近6万篇德语报纸中关于滑坡的新闻报道（涵盖约5500个新闻事件），并将其与各国滑坡易发性外部指标进行比较，揭示了媒体对国际灾害报道的不平等现象，如南欧和西欧的过度报道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻是创建或丰富灾害数据库、研究媒体注意力动态的宝贵信息来源，但需要过滤、地理定位和验证。本研究旨在通过分析德语报纸中全球滑坡事件的报道，揭示媒体关注度的地域不平等。

Method: 收集25年间约6万篇德语滑坡新闻文章，识别出约5500个新闻事件，并与各国滑坡易发性的外部指标进行对比分析。

Result: 发现德语媒体对南欧和西欧的滑坡事件存在过度报道，而对其他高易发区域报道不足，揭示了媒体注意力在地域上的显著不平等。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解媒体对国际灾害的报道偏差提供了实证依据，有助于推动进一步研究媒体注意力不平等现象及其对灾害认知和资源分配的影响。

Abstract: Landslides often hit newsstands due to their destructive and potentially fatal effects. News are a valuable source of information for creating or enriching disaster databases and for expediting media-based studies of the dynamics of media attention. To accomplish that, news datasets must be filtered, geolocated and validated. This paper focuses on how landslides around the world are reported in German newspapers. We analyse almost 60k news articles about 5.5k news events in a 25-year period, compare it with external measures of countries' susceptibility to landslides and provide insights, e.g.~the overreporting of Southern and Western Europe, to foment further studies on inequalities in media attention to international disasters.

</details>


### [157] [How Good LLMs Are at Answering Bangla Medical Visual Questions? Dataset and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18111)
*Rafid Ahmed,Intesar Tahmid,Mir Sazzat Hossain,Tasnimul Hossain Tomal,Md Fahim,Md Farhad Alam Bhuiyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BanglaMedVQA，首个孟加拉语医学视觉问答基准数据集，并评估了当前基础模型，发现即使最先进的模型（如Gemini、GPT-4.1 mini）在孟加拉语MedVQA任务上表现极差，突显低资源语言的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语是全球使用最广泛的语言之一，但缺乏医学视觉问答（MedVQA）基准数据集，现有MedVQA研究主要集中于英语等高资源语言。

Method: 构建了BanglaMedVQA数据集，包含经过临床验证的图像-问题-答案三元组，并对当前主流基础模型（包括闭源和开源模型）进行了系统性评估。

Result: 所有模型在孟加拉语MedVQA上表现显著低于英语基准；顶尖模型（Gemini、GPT-4.1 mini）在专业诊断问题上无法给出准确答案；某些开源模型（如Gemma-3）在通用类别上偶有超越，但仍难以处理临床复杂问题。

Conclusion: 当前基础模型在孟加拉语医学视觉问答任务上存在严重局限性，亟需开发针对低资源语言的高质量评估方法和专用模型。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) have enabled general-purpose systems to demonstrate promising capabilities in complex reasoning tasks, including those in the medical domain. Medical Visual Question Answering (MedVQA) has particularly benefited from these developments. However, despite Bangla being one of the most widely spoken languages globally, there exists no established MedVQA benchmark for it. To address this gap, we introduce BanglaMedVQA, a dataset comprising clinically validated image-question-answer pairs, along with a comprehensive evaluation of current foundation models on this resource. Consistent with prior findings that report low performance of current models on English MedVQA benchmarks, our analysis reveals that Bangla performance is substantially lower, reflecting the challenges inherent to low-resource languages. Even top-performing models such as Gemini and GPT-4.1 mini fail to accurately answer specialized diagnostic questions, indicating severe limitations in fine-grained medical reasoning. Although certain open-source models, such as Gemma-3, occasionally outperform these models in general categories, they too struggle with clinically complex questions, underscoring the urgent need for top-notch evaluation method.

</details>


### [158] [iPOE: Interpretable Prompt Optimization via Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18113)
*Jiahui Li,Sean Papay,Roman Klinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: iPOE是一种可解释的提示优化策略，通过自动从解释中生成指南来指导提示优化过程，在四个数据集上相比无指南提示提升高达31%，相比随机指南提示提升高达35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法通常视为离散搜索问题，优化结果缺乏透明度，无法解释为何以及何处提示变更带来性能提升，这与人类标注任务中精心设计标注指南的做法形成对比。

Method: 提出iPOE方法，通过自动从标注决策的解释（自动生成或人工提供）中创建指南，并对指南集执行删除、添加、打乱、合并等系列操作进行优化，最终提示包含指导标注的指南，使优化过程透明化。

Result: 在四个数据集上，iPOE相比无指南提示提升高达31%，相比随机选择指南提示提升高达35%；LLM生成的解释可替代人工解释。

Conclusion: iPOE通过可解释的指南驱动提示优化，显著提升性能的同时保持决策过程透明，降低了提示优化的门槛，尤其适用于需要专业知识的挑战性领域。

Abstract: Prompt optimization has often been framed as a discrete search problem to find high-performing and robust instructions for an LLM. However, the search result might not make it transparent why and where specific prompt changes lead to performance gains. This is in contrast to how humans are instructed for annotation tasks. Here, researchers carefully design annotation guidelines, leading to enhanced annotation consistency. Our paper aims at joining these two approaches and introduces iPOE, a novel interpretable prompt optimization strategy via explanations. We guide the prompt optimization process by automatically created guidelines from explanations of annotation decisions (either automatically generated or from humans). This set of guidelines is furthermore optimized by as series of operations, including removing, adding, shuffling, and merging. The resulting prompt includes guidelines that instruct the annotation, making the decision process of the LLM and the optimization transparent. It therefore supports also laypeople in the area of prompt optimization, particularly in challenging domains requiring expertise. In our experiments on four datasets, we find that iPOE can improves over prompts without guidelines and with random selected guidelines by up to $31\%$ and $35\%$, respectively. Moreover, LLM explanations can replace human explanations in the proposed method.

</details>


### [159] [FOL2NS: Generating Natural Sentences from First-Order Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18155)
*Mei Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FOL2NS框架，通过神经符号方法合成一阶逻辑公式并生成自然语言句子，能够处理深层嵌套结构（不同量词深度），实验表明能生成格式良好的模板和流畅语句，但在结构复杂度增加时面临语义精确性和生成自然性的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式语言到自然语言的翻译是NLP的基础挑战，驱动语义解析、定理验证和问答等下游应用。现有语料库难以捕捉深层嵌套结构和变化量词深度的FOL公式。

Method: 提出FOL2NS神经符号框架，结合规则驱动模块与微调语言模型，生成合成一阶逻辑公式并转换为自然语言表达。通过字符级分析和整体性能指标系统评估框架能力。

Result: FOL2NS能可靠生成格式良好的模板和流畅语句，但在结构复杂度增加时难以实现精确的语义表示和自然的生成效果。

Conclusion: FOL2NS在生成结构良好的FOL-自然语言转换方面有效，但复杂结构下的语义精确性和自然性仍需改进。

Abstract: Translating formal language into natural language is a foundational challenge in NLP, driving various downstream applications in semantic parsing, theorem validation, and question answering. In this study, we introduce First-Order Logic to Natural Sentence (FOL2NS), a neurosymbolic framework designed to generate synthetic FOL formulas and convert them into natural human expressions. It handles deeply nested structures with varying quantifier depths (QD), which are rarely captured by existing corpora. By combining rule-driven modules with fine-tuned language models, FOL2NS enhances the diversity and coverage of the generated samples. In our experiments, we systematically evaluate the framework's capabilities through both character-level analysis and overall performance metrics. Experimental results show that FOL2NS can reliably produce well-formed templates and fluent statements, but it faces challenges in achieving precise semantic representations and natural generation as structural complexity increases.

</details>


### [160] [Context Memorization for Efficient Long Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18226)
*Yasuyuki Okoshi,Hao Mark Chen,Guanxi Lu,Hongxiang Fan,Masato Motomura,Daichi Fujiki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力状态记忆（attention-state memory），一种无需训练的方法，通过将前缀的注意力状态外化为轻量级、基于查表的记忆，解决了前缀增强推理中前缀影响衰减和注意力计算随长度线性扩展的问题。在ManyICLBench和NBA基准上，该方法在降低延迟的同时，以更小内存超越全注意力的RAG性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM应用依赖长条件前缀来控制模型行为，但前缀增强推理存在两个结构性限制：1) 前缀的影响力随着生成过程逐渐衰减；2) 前缀上的注意力计算量与其长度呈线性扩展。现有方法要么在推理时仍保留前缀注意力，要么需要密集的梯度训练且不适合前缀更新。

Method: 提出注意力状态记忆（attention-state memory），一种无需训练的方法。将前缀外化为轻量级、基于查表的记忆结构，该记忆存储前缀与查询token之间预先计算好的注意力状态，从而在推理时避免对原始前缀进行完整的注意力计算。

Result: 在ManyICLBench上使用LLaMA-3.1-8B，在1K-8K内存预算内，该方法相比上下文学习提升了准确率，同时在8K长度下将注意力延迟降低了1.36倍。在NBA基准上，仅使用全注意力RAG 20%的内存开销，就超越了其性能。

Conclusion: 注意力状态记忆是一种无需训练、高效的替代方案，通过外部化前缀注意力状态，同时缓解了前缀影响衰减和计算扩展性问题，在性能和效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) applications increasingly rely on long conditioning prefixes to control model behavior at inference time. While prefix-augmented inference is effective, it incurs two structural limitations: i) the prefix's influence fades as generation proceeds, and ii) attention computation over the prefix scales linearly with its length. Existing approaches either keep the prefix in attention while compressing it, or internalize it into model parameters through gradient-based training. The former still attends to the prefix at inference, while the latter is training-intensive and ill-suited to prefix updates. To address these issues, we propose attention-state memory, a training-free approach that externalizes the prefix into a lightweight, lookup-based memory of precomputed attention states between prefix and query tokens. On ManyICLBench with LLaMA-3.1-8B, our method improves accuracy over in-context learning at 1K-8K memory budgets while reducing attention latency by 1.36x at 8K, and surpasses full-attention RAG performance on NBA benchmark using only 20% of its memory footprint.

</details>


### [161] [SomaliWeb v1: A Quality-Filtered Somali Web Corpus with a Matched Tokenizer and a Public Language-Identification Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18232)
*Khalid Yusuf Dahir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SomaliWeb v1，第一个专门面向索马里语（Cushitic语系，约2500万 speakers）的高质量预训练语料库，包含819,322篇文档（~3.03亿 tokens），配套BPE-16K分词器及首个索马里语语言识别基准。研究揭示了现有多语言发布版（如HPLT v2）中严重的质量缺陷：17.3%的字节级重复、56.1%的文档含可修复的mojibake乱码、10.7%的近重复文档。新分词器相比GPT-4的cl100k_base在FLORES-200上减少40.2%的token数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 索马里语虽有约2500万使用者，但此前缺乏公开的专用预训练语料库、配套分词器和语言识别基准。现有索马里语文本分散在多语言数据集中（如HPLT v2、CC100等）或为Hugging Face上少量未文档化的上传，质量参差不齐且无法复现，阻碍了该语言NLP研究的发展。

Method: 从三个上游来源（HPLT v2、CC100、索马里语维基百科）经过六阶段可复现流水线构建：(1) 语言识别过滤（使用多个生产级标识器）；(2) 去重（字节级精确去重、MinHash近似去重）；(3) mojibake乱码检测与修复；(4) 质量过滤（长度、符号比例等启发式规则）；(5) BPE-16K分词器训练；(6) 构建首个索马里语语言标识器对比基准。

Result: 得到819,322篇文档/约3.03亿tokens的高质量语料库。发现HPLT v2中17.3%为完全重复文档，56.1%含可修复mojibake，10.7%为近重复。新分词器在FLORES-200索马里语devtest上比GPT-4的cl100k_base减少40.2%的token数。

Conclusion: SomaliWeb v1填补了索马里语NLP资源的空白，为低资源语言的数据处理提供了可复现的质量控制基准；明确指出现有公开语料库存在严重质量缺陷，强调专门化语料构建的必要性；分词器效率显著提升，但下游语言模型困惑度评估留待后续工作。

Abstract: Somali is a Cushitic language of the Horn of Africa with ~25 million speakers, yet no documented dedicated Somali pretraining corpus with a companion tokenizer and language-identification benchmark has been publicly released. Existing Somali text appears either inside multilingual distributions (HPLT v2, CC100, MADLAD-400, OSCAR, mC4) or in small, undocumented Somali-only uploads on Hugging Face. We introduce SomaliWeb v1, a quality-filtered Somali corpus of 819,322 documents (~303M tokens) built from three upstream sources (HPLT v2, CC100, Somali Wikipedia) through a six-stage reproducible pipeline. We release (i) the corpus, (ii) a matched BPE-16K tokenizer, and (iii) the first public side-by-side Somali benchmark of three production language identifiers. Our measurements reveal concrete quality defects in existing distributions: HPLT v2's "cleaned" Somali release retains 17.3% byte-exact duplicates, 56.1% of its documents contain fixable mojibake, and 10.7% of its byte-unique documents are near-duplicates at Jaccard tau=0.80. Our BPE-16K tokenizer emits 40.2% fewer tokens than GPT-4's cl100k_base on FLORES-200 Somali devtest as a tokenizer-level measurement; downstream language-model perplexity comparisons are deferred to a follow-up release.

</details>


### [162] [Multilingual jailbreaking of LLMs using low-resource languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18239)
*Dylan Marx,Marcel Dunaiski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了利用低资源非洲语言（南非语、斯瓦希里语、科萨语、祖鲁语）进行多轮对话以绕过商业LLM安全机制的方法。单轮翻译攻击无效，而多轮对话实现了显著的有害响应率；人工红队测试比自动化方法获得更高的越狱成功率；翻译质量是决定低资源语言越狱成功的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在面对越狱攻击时仍然脆弱，现有研究多聚焦于高资源语言（如英语），而对低资源语言的多轮对话越狱能力缺乏系统评估，特别是非洲语言的环境尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将现有数据集的提示翻译成四种低资源非洲语言，对ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek、Gemini和Grok等商业LLM进行自动化测试和母语者人工红队测试，对比单轮翻译攻击与多轮对话攻击的效果。

Result: 单轮翻译攻击无效；多轮对话中英语有害响应率52.7%-83.6%，南非语60.0%-78.2%，斯瓦希里语41.8%-70.9%。人工红队相比自动化方法将平均越狱率从59.8%提升至75.8%，其中南非语提升+20.0%，祖鲁语+12.7%，科萨语+12.3%，斯瓦希里语仅+1%。

Conclusion: LLM在多语言环境下的安全漏洞依然存在，翻译质量是低资源语言越狱攻击成功的关键决定因素；低翻译质量限制了越狱成功率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attempts that circumvent safety guardrails. We investigate whether multi-turn conversations using low-resource African languages (Afrikaans, Kiswahili, isiXhosa, and isiZulu) can bypass safety mechanisms across commercial LLMs. We translated prompts from existing datasets and evaluated ChatGPT, Claude, DeepSeek, Gemini, and Grok through automated testing and human red-teaming with native speakers. Single-turn translation attacks proved ineffective, while multi-turn conversations achieved English harmful response rates from 52.7% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 83.6% (GPT-4o-mini), Afrikaans from 60.0% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 78.2% (GPT-4o-mini), and Kiswahili from 41.8% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 70.9% (DeepSeek). Human red-teaming increased jailbreak rates compared to automated methods. Over all evaluated languages, the average jailbreak rate increased from 59.8% to 75.8%, with improvements of +20.0% (Afrikaans), +12.7% (isiZulu), +12.3% (isiXhosa), and +1% (Kiswahili), demonstrating that poor translation quality limits jailbreak success. These findings suggest that vulnerabilities in LLMs persist in multilingual contexts and that translation quality is the critical factor determining jailbreak success in low-resource languages.

</details>


### [163] [Knowledge-to-Verification: Exploring RLVR for LLMs in Knowledge-Intensive Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18261)
*Zhonghang Yuan,Zhefan Wang,Fang Hu,Zihong Chen,Jinzhe Li,Gang Li,Jie Ying,Huanjun Kong,Songyang Zhang,Nanqing Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出K2V框架，通过自动化可验证数据合成和推理过程验证，将RLVR扩展到知识密集型领域，实验表明能提升LLM推理能力而不显著损害通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR主要应用于数学和编程领域，未有效探索知识密集型领域，且仅关注最终答案正确性，存在推理过程有缺陷和奖励信号稀疏的问题。

Method: 提出Knowledge-to-Verification (K2V)框架，通过自动化可验证数据合成将RLVR扩展到知识密集型领域，同时实现对LLM推理过程的验证。

Result: 实验表明K2V能增强LLM在知识密集型领域的推理能力，且不会显著损害模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: 将自动化数据合成与推理验证相结合是增强模型在更广泛领域能力的有前景方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has demonstrated promising potential to enhance the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in domains such as mathematics and coding. However, its applications on knowledge-intensive domains have not been effectively explored due to the scarcity of high-quality verifiable data. Furthermore, current RLVR focuses solely on the correctness of final answers, leading to the limitations of flawed reasoning and sparse reward signals. In this work, we propose Knowledge-to-Verification (K2V), a framework that extends RLVR to knowledge-intensive domains through automated verifiable data synthesis, while enabling verification of the LLM's reasoning process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that K2V enhances the reasoning of LLM in knowledge-intensive domains without significantly compromising the model's general capabilities. This study also suggests that integrating automated data synthesis with reasoning verification is a promising direction to enhance model capabilities in these broader domains. Code is available at https://github.com/SeedScientist/K2V.

</details>


### [164] [Infini-News: Efficiently Queryable Access to 1.3 Billion Processed Common Crawl News Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18337)
*Ruggero Marino Lazzaroni,Jana Lasser,Kirill Solovev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Infini-News 是一个面向整个 CC-News 语料库（2016年8月至今，涵盖13.5亿篇文章）的检索工具包和索引，通过文本清洗、语言检测、地理归因和 Infini-gram 后缀数组索引，实现亚秒级任意文本模式搜索，极大降低了大规模跨时空新闻研究的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模新闻语料库要么因商业许可和成本问题受限，要么如 CC-News 需要 TB 级存储和密集计算资源，阻碍了计算社会科学和 NLP 领域的研究。

Method: (1) 提取、清洗并解析超过13.5亿篇文章的结构化元数据；(2) 使用三种前沿语言分类器（GlotLID、lingua、CommonLingua）进行语言检测，并结合多源地理归因方法确定83.4%文章的国家来源（覆盖222个国家）；(3) 构建 Infini-gram 后缀数组索引，支持亚秒级任意文本模式搜索。

Result: 成功构建了覆盖整个 CC-News 存档的检索索引，支持亚秒级文本模式搜索，并实现了高覆盖率的语言检测和地理归因（83.4%文章归因到222个国家）。

Conclusion: Infini-News 降低了进行纵向、跨国媒体研究的门槛，为计算社会科学和 NLP 领域提供了便捷、高效的大规模新闻语料库访问途径。

Abstract: Large-scale news corpora support a wide range of research in Computational Social Science and NLP, yet access remains constrained: commercial archives impose prohibitive costs and licensing restrictions, while open alternatives like Common Crawl's CC-News require terabyte-scale storage and computationally intensive processing. We present Infini-News, a retrieval toolkit and index for the entire CC-News archive from August 2016 to the latest available snapshot. Our contributions are threefold. First, we extract, clean the text, and parse the structured metadata of over 1.35B articles. Second, we enrich the corpus with language detection using three frontier language classifiers (GlotLID, lingua, and CommonLingua), and with multi-source geographic attribution that resolves a country of origin for 83.4% of articles across 222 countries. Third, we construct Infini-gram indexes: suffix-array structures that let researchers search the full archive for arbitrary text patterns in sub-second time. Together, these resources lower the barrier to longitudinal, cross-national media research.

</details>


### [165] [Presupposition and Reasoning in Conditionals: A Theory-Based Study of Humans and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18352)
*Tara Azin,Yongan Yu,Raj Singh,Olessia Jouravlev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对比人类与LLM在条件句预设投射任务上的表现，发现人类整合概率与语用线索进行判断，而LLM与人类模式存在差异：与人类评分最匹配的模型缺乏连贯语用推理，推理能力更强的模型反而产生更不类人的判断，表明LLM的表现可能源于表面模式匹配而非语用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件句中的预设投射是意义与语用理论的核心问题，但大语言模型在这一任务上的表现尚未得到系统评估。

Method: 通过平行行为研究，在控制前件与投射预设关系的规范化条件句数据集上，收集120名参与者和4个LLM的可能性评分，并采用基于语言学清单的LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估模型推理。

Result: 人类整合概率与语用线索；LLM与人类模式的对齐程度不一；与人类评分最匹配的模型缺乏连贯的语用推理，而推理能力更强的模型产生更不类人的判断。

Conclusion: LLM在此类任务上的表现可能源自表面模式匹配而非语用能力，强调了基于语言学理论的基准对于比较人类与模型的重要性。

Abstract: Presupposition projection in conditionals is central to theories of meaning and pragmatics, yet it remains largely unevaluated in large language models. We address this gap through a parallel behavioral study comparing human judgments and LLM predictions on a normed dataset of conditional sentences that controls the relation between the antecedent and the projected presupposition. We collect likelihood ratings from 120 participants and four LLMs under matched contextual conditions. Results show that humans integrate probabilistic and pragmatic cues in their judgment, whereas LLMs show variable alignment with human patterns. Using a linguistically motivated checklist within an LLM-as-a-Judge framework, we further evaluate model reasoning. We observe models that best match human ratings often lack coherent pragmatic reasoning, while models with stronger reasoning produce less human-like judgments. These findings suggest that LLMs' performance on such tasks may result from surface pattern matching rather than pragmatic competence. Our findings highlight the importance of benchmarks grounded in linguistic theory for comparing humans and models.

</details>


### [166] [SkillsVote: Lifecycle Governance of Agent Skills from Collection, Recommendation to Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18401)
*Hongyi Liu,Haoyan Yang,Tao Jiang,Bo Tang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillsVote 是一个面向 LLM Agent 技能的全生命周期治理框架，涵盖技能收集、推荐与演化环节。它通过大规模开源语料分析构建结构化技能库，在执行前后分别进行智能检索与轨迹分解，并采用证据门控机制只接纳可验证的成功发现进行更新。实验表明，该框架在不更新模型参数的情况下，通过治理外部技能库显著提升了多类基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 LLM Agent 留下的轨迹虽可作为可复用经验，但原始轨迹噪声大、难以管理。现有开源技能生态存在冗余、质量不均、环境敏感等问题，且不加区分的更新会污染未来上下文。因此需要一个系统化的生命周期治理框架来管控 Agent Skills 的质量与演化。

Method: 1) 将 Agent Skills 定义为"可执行脚本 + 不可执行程序指南"的经验模式；2) 对百万级开源语料进行环境需求、质量和可验证性画像，合成可验证技能的任务；3) 执行前，通过智能体库搜索检索结构化技能库以暴露指令性技能上下文；4) 执行后，将轨迹分解为技能关联的子任务，归因结果信号（技能使用、Agent 探索、环境），仅接纳成功的可复用发现进行证据门控更新。

Result: 离线演化使 GPT-5.2 在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上提升最多 7.9 个百分点；在线演化使 SWE-Bench Pro 提升最多 2.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 经过治理的外部技能库能够在系统控制曝光、归因和保存的前提下，提升冻结 Agent 的性能而无需更新模型参数。SkillsVote 证明了结构化、可治理的技能生命周期管理是提升 LLM Agent 长期能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents leave traces that could become reusable experience, but raw trajectories are noisy and hard to govern. We treat Agent Skills as an experience schema that couples executable scripts, with non-executable guidance on procedures. Yet open skill ecosystems contain redundant, uneven, environment-sensitive artifacts, and indiscriminate updates can pollute future context. We present SkillsVote, a lifecycle-governance framework for Agent Skills from collection and recommendation to evolution. SkillsVote profiles a million-scale open-source corpus for environment requirements, quality, and verifiability, then synthesizes tasks for verifiable skills. Before execution, SkillsVote performs agentic library search over structured skill library to expose instructional skill context. After execution, it decomposes trajectories into skill-linked subtasks, attributes outcomes to skill use, agent exploration, environment, and result signals, and admits only successful reusable discoveries to evidence-gated updates. In our evaluation, offline evolution improves GPT-5.2 on Terminal-Bench 2.0 by up to 7.9 pp, while online evolution improves SWE-Bench Pro by up to 2.6 pp. Overall, governed external skill libraries can improve frozen agents without model updates when systems control exposure, credit, and preservation.

</details>


### [167] [EvoMemBench: Benchmarking Agent Memory from a Self-Evolving Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18421)
*Yuyao Wang,Zhongjian Zhang,Mo Chi,Kaichi Yu,Yuhan Li,Miao Peng,Bing Tong,Chen Zhang,Yan Zhou,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoMemBench基准，系统评估LLM智能体的记忆机制，发现当前记忆系统尚未达到通用解决方案，长上下文基线仍具竞争力，记忆在不同场景下效果各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准主要评估推理、规划和执行能力，但记忆（存储、更新、检索信息）作为关键能力缺乏系统评估方法，亟需统一的评估框架。

Method: 从自演化视角出发，沿记忆范围（episode内/跨episode）和记忆内容（知识导向/执行导向）两个维度构建EvoMemBench基准，在标准化协议下比较15种记忆方法与强长上下文基线。

Result: 当前记忆系统远非通用解决方案：长上下文基线仍极具竞争力；记忆仅在当前上下文不足或任务困难时最有效；没有单一记忆形式在所有设置中一致有效；检索式方法在知识密集型场景中表现强，而过程性/长期记忆方法在执行导向任务中更有效（当存储经验匹配任务结构时）。

Conclusion: EvoMemBench为未来更有效的LLM智能体记忆系统研究提供了统一的评估平台。

Abstract: Recent benchmarks for Large Language Model (LLM) agents mainly evaluate reasoning, planning, and execution. However, memory is also essential for agents, as it enables them to store, update, and retrieve information over time. This ability remains under-evaluated, largely because existing benchmarks do not provide a systematic way to assess memory mechanisms. In this paper, we study agent memory from a self-evolving perspective and introduce EvoMemBench, a unified benchmark organized along two axes: memory scope (in-episode vs. cross-episode) and memory content (knowledge-oriented vs. execution-oriented). We compare 15 representative memory methods with strong long-context baselines under a standardized protocol. Results show that current memory systems are still far from a general solution: long-context baselines remain highly competitive, memory helps most when the current context is insufficient or tasks are difficult, and no single memory form works consistently across all settings. Retrieval-based methods remain strong for knowledge-intensive settings, whereas procedural and long-term memory methods are more effective for execution-oriented tasks when their stored experience matches the task structure. We hope EvoMemBench facilitates future research on more effective memory systems for LLM-based agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/DSAIL-Memory/EvoMemBench.

</details>


### [168] [From BERT to T5: A Study of Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18462)
*Mei Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在命名实体识别(NER)任务上微调两种预训练模型：BERT（编码器-仅模型+简单分类头）和T5（序列到序列模型+少样本提示）。在7类标签和3类简化标签方案下，BERT使用加权交叉熵损失，T5采用两种验证策略，并通过消融实验和错误分析比较两者在序列标注任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NER是NLP应用中的关键预处理步骤，本文旨在对比编码器-仅模型（BERT）与序列到序列模型（T5）在NER任务上的表现差异，为实际应用中的模型选择提供依据。

Method: 采用两种微调策略：(1) BERT作为编码器-仅模型，添加简单分类头，使用加权交叉熵损失；(2) T5作为序列到序列模型，使用少样本提示微调。在原始7类标签和简化3类标签方案下进行实验，包含两种验证策略和超参数消融研究。

Result: 通过多项性能指标对比了BERT和T5在NER任务上的表现，分析了BERT的常见错误模式以及两种模型的性能差异。

Conclusion: 本研究通过系统比较BERT和T5在NER任务上的表现，为序列标注任务中的模型选型提供了参考依据，并为后续实际应用场景奠定了基础。

Abstract: Named entity recognition (NER) has been one of the essential preliminary steps in modern NLP applications. This report focuses on implementing the NER task on finetuning two pretrained models: (i) an encoder-only model (BERT) with a simple classification head, and (ii) a sequence-to-sequence model (T5) with few-shot prompts. Under the original 7-class tag and 3-class simplified tag schemes, BERT is applied a weighted cross-entropy for training loss, and T5 is fine-tuned with two validation strategies. It also conducted an ablation study with different hyperparameters. Moreover, the related analysis provides valuable insights into common errors in BERT and the two models' performance. Based on a bunch of performance metrics, this report aims to compare the above two architectures and explore their abilities in the sequence labelling task, laying the groundwork for further practical use cases.

</details>


### [169] [Vector RAG vs LLM-Compiled Wiki: A Preregistered Comparison on a Small Multi-Domain Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18490)
*Theodore O. Cochran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 比较了单轮向量RAG和LLM编译的Markdown Wiki两种方法，用于帮助LLM回答小规模研究语料库的问题。Wiki在跨论文连接方面更好，但RAG在单事实查询上更优，且成本更低。两种架构在不同维度上各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何更有效地帮助LLM在小规模研究语料库上进行知识问答，比较两种主流方法（RAG和Wiki）的性能与成本差异。

Method: 预注册实验，使用相同答案生成模型，对比单轮向量RAG系统和LLM编译的Markdown Wiki在13个问题、24篇论文上的表现，由盲审LLM评分。额外进行两项探索性分析：声明级引用检查和基于分解的RAG变体。

Result: Wiki在跨论文连接方面明显更好，但组织优势经评审调整后不显著；RAG在单事实查询上达到预注册检验标准；Wiki查询侧token消耗远高于RAG，无法通过更便宜的查询回收前期构建成本；分解式RAG变体恢复了Wiki的大部分跨论文综合优势，但未恢复声明级引用支持的优势。

Conclusion: 基础研究综合并不是单一能力，不同系统在证据组织、声明引用支持和运行成本三个维度上各有优劣，没有一种架构在所有维度上都是最优的。

Abstract: We preregistered a comparison of two ways to help an LLM answer questions over a small research corpus: a single-round Vector RAG system and an LLM-compiled markdown wiki. Both systems answered the same 13 questions over 24 papers using the same answer-generating model, and their answers were scored by blinded LLM judges.
  The wiki scored much better at connecting findings across papers, but its advantage in answer organization was not strong after judge adjustment. RAG met the preregistered test for single-fact lookup questions. The clean query-side cost result went against the expected wiki advantage: under the tested setup, the wiki used far more query tokens than RAG, so it could not recover any upfront build cost through cheaper queries.
  Two exploratory analyses changed how we interpret the result. First, claim-level citation checking favored the wiki: its cited pages more often supported the exact claims being made, even though RAG scored better on the overall groundedness rubric. Second, a decomposition-based RAG variant recovered most of the wiki's advantage on cross-paper synthesis at lower LLM-token cost, but it did not recover the wiki advantage in claim-by-claim citation support.
  The main conclusion is that grounded research synthesis is not a single capability. Systems can differ in how well they organize evidence, how well their citations support each claim, and how much they cost to run. In this study, no architecture was best on all three.

</details>


### [170] [Implicit Hierarchical GRPO: Decoupling Tool Invocation from Execution for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18500)
*Li Wang,Xiaohan Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Zipeng Zhang,Jinyang Wu,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次提出将工具调用与执行解耦的问题，并引入延迟执行与显式控制来增强工具集成推理。提出层次化控制框架和IH-GRPO算法，在多个数学推理基准上取得1.87%-2.53%的绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将工具调用与立即执行紧密耦合，这会打断LLM的推理连贯性并限制其表达能力，降低推理性能。

Method: 1. 形式化定义工具调用与执行解耦问题；2. 提出延迟执行与显式控制的TIR框架；3. 设计层次化控制框架；4. 理论推导出替代损失函数，使隐式层次策略学习等价于显式层次策略的行为，得到IH-GRPO算法。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B上，于6个域外数学推理基准中分别取得1.87%、2.16%、2.53%的绝对提升，且在其它领域也取得一致性能增益。

Conclusion: 解耦工具调用与执行能有效提升LLM的推理性能，IH-GRPO算法验证了该思路的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly leveraged tool invocation to enhance their reasoning capabilities. However, existing approaches typically tightly couple tool invocation with immediate execution. Such immediate tool interaction may disrupt the reasoning coherence of LLMs and constrain their expressivity, ultimately degrading reasoning performance. To this end, for the first time, we propose and formalize the problem of decoupling tool invocation from execution during reasoning, and introduce delayed execution with explicit control to enhance tool-integrated reasoning (TIR). Furthermore, we propose a hierarchical control framework and theoretically derive a surrogate loss that enables an implicitly hierarchical policy to learn behavior equivalent to that of an explicit hierarchical policy, leading to the proposed IH-GRPO algorithm. Extensive experiments on IH-GRPO achieve absolute improvements of 1.87\%, 2.16\%, and 2.53\% on Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B across six out-of-domain mathematical reasoning benchmarks over the strongest baseline method, while also yielding consistent performance gains in other domains. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/IH-GRPO-01.

</details>


### [171] [Easier to Judge than to Find: Predicting In-Context Learning Success for Demonstration Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18512)
*Haochun Wang,Chaofen Yang,Jiatong Liu,Jingbo Wang,Zewen Qiang,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSP框架，通过“先采样再判断”策略解决上下文学习（ICL）中演示样本选择成本过高的问题。该方法先随机采样演示样本评估查询难度，训练轻量级路由器预测难度，再为不同难度级别训练专用评估器。推理时在预算约束下执行“接受即停止”策略，并在无合适上下文时发出诊断标签。在五个分类数据集上，DiSP相比强基线平均准确率提升最高3.4%，端到端加速最高23倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL对演示样本高度敏感，但选择最优演示样本的组合空间巨大，成本极高。现有方法侧重于搜索最优演示集，而作者认为判断一个查询-上下文对是否有效比搜索最优解更廉价、更通用。

Method: 提出DiSP框架：(1) 对每个训练查询随机采样演示样本进行试验，估计成功率；(2) 训练轻量级路由器根据查询预测难度；(3) 为不同难度级别训练专用评估器来判断演示样本质量。推理时采用“接受即停止”策略，在预算约束下进行判断，无合适上下文时发出诊断风险标签。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和Qwen-2.5-7B上对五个分类数据集进行测试，DiSP平均准确率最佳，比强学习选择基线提升最高3.4%，端到端加速比最高达23倍。

Conclusion: 验证了“判断比搜索更容易”的核心见解，DiSP通过将查询按难度分层并采用预算约束下的停止策略，在保证准确率的同时大幅提升效率。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is highly sensitive to which demonstrations appear in the prompt, but selecting them is expensive because the space of possible demonstration contexts and combinations is enormous. We argue that demonstration selection is \emph{easier to judge than to find}: predicting whether a specific query--context pair $(q,D)$ will succeed is cheaper and more general than searching for an optimal $D^\star$. Based on this insight, we propose DiSP, a sample-and-judge framework that stratifies queries by difficulty. DiSP runs random demonstration trials to estimate success rate of each training query, trains a lightweight router to predict difficulty from the query, and trains level-specific judges for sampled demonstrations. At inference, DiSP performs stop-on-acceptance judging under an explicit budget, emitting diagnostic risk tags when no suitable context is found. Across five classification datasets with Llama~3--8B and Qwen~2.5--7B, DiSP achieves the best average accuracy, improving over strong learned selection baselines by up to 3.4\%, while achieving up to $23\times$ end-to-end wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [172] [STT-Arena: A More Realistic Environment for Tool-Using with Spatio-Temporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18548)
*Tingfeng Hui,Hao Xu,Pengyu Zhu,Hongsheng Xin,Kun Zhan,Sen Su,Chunxiao Liu,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出STT-Arena基准（227个任务，9种时空冲突类型，4种可解级别），评估前沿LLM在时空动态推理中的自适应重规划能力。最强模型Claude-4.6-Opus准确率<40%，识别三种失败模式，提出STT-Agent-4B（在线RL+迭代轨迹精炼）超越前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态基准主要检测LLM是否及时感知时间变化，缺乏对时空动态下自适应重规划能力的系统评估。现实智能体应用需要在任务中途遭遇干扰时重新规划并调整策略，这一互补挑战尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建STT-Arena基准（227个交互任务，9种时空冲突类型，4种可解级别），在可执行环境中注入时空触发器使计划失效。通过系统分析失败轨迹识别三种错误模式，提出迭代轨迹精炼技术消除训练数据中的失败模式，结合在线RL训练STT-Agent-4B。

Result: 前沿模型在STT-Arena上表现不佳：Claude-4.6-Opus准确率<40%。识别三种失败模式：陈旧状态执行、动态触发器误诊、缺乏适应后验证。STT-Agent-4B（仅4B参数量）超越所有评估的前沿LLM。

Conclusion: 时空动态推理对当前LLM仍是根本性难题；通过系统分析失败模式并针对性设计训练方法（迭代轨迹精炼+在线RL），小模型可超越大模型在该任务上的表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed in real-world agentic applications must be capable of replanning and adapting when mid-task disruptions invalidate their prior decisions. Existing dynamic benchmarks primarily measure whether LLMs can detect temporal changes in a timely manner, leaving the complementary challenge of adaptive replanning under spatio-temporal dynamics largely unexplored. We introduce STT-Arena (Spatio-Temporal Tool-Use Arena), a benchmark of 227 high-quality interactive tasks spanning nine spatio-temporal conflict types and four solvability levels. Each task is grounded in a realistic, executable environment equipped with injected spatio-temporal triggers that can abruptly invalidate an ongoing plan, forcing the model to detect the state shift and construct a revised execution strategy. Extensive evaluation of frontier LLMs reveals that even the SOTA proprietary models, including Claude-4.6-Opus, achieves less than 40\% overall accuracies, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of spatio-temporal dynamic reasoning. Systematic analysis of failure trajectories uncovers three recurring error modes of existing models: Stale-State Execution, Misdiagnosis of Dynamic Triggers, and Missing Post-Adaptation Verification. Guided by these findings, we propose an iterative trajectory refinement technique that eliminates these failure patterns from training data, and combine it with online RL to produce STT-Agent-4B which outperforms frontier LLMs on STT-Arena.

</details>


### [173] [Monitoring the Internal Monologue: Probe Trajectories Reveal Reasoning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18549)
*Maciej Chrabąszcz,Aleksander Szymczyk,Marcin Sendera,Tomasz Trzciński,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过探针轨迹（probe trajectories）来监控大型推理模型（LRMs）的思维链（CoT）过程。研究发现，完整轨迹上的未来行为可区分性优于单点静态预测，通过提取波动性、趋势和稳态等信号处理特征可显著提升分离效果。模板训练数据与动态生成数据性能接近，最大池化算子优于平均池化和最后token方法，在多个数据集和模型上可达95% AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRMs的CoT推理虽然为安全监控提供了新机会，但CoT并不总是忠实于模型的最终输出，这削弱了其作为监控工具的可靠性。因此需要从隐藏表示层面探究是否可以通过提示和CoT表示预测模型未来行为。

Method: 在每个生成token上评估探针，构建探针轨迹（概念概率在推理过程中的连续演化）。提取信号处理特征（波动性、趋势、稳态行为）刻画时间动态特性。比较模板训练数据与动态生成数据的效果，并对比不同池化操作（平均池化、最后token、最大池化）的性能。

Result: 完整轨迹上的未来行为可区分性优于单点静态预测；模板训练数据与动态生成模型响应性能接近；最大池化算子达到95% AUROC，而平均池化和最后token方法几乎退化为随机性能。在四个数据集和四个推理模型上验证了轨迹特征编码了任务特定动态，改善了结果可分离性。

Conclusion: 探针轨迹可作为监控LRM行为的补充框架，通过分析推理过程中的连续表示演化来预测模型未来行为，为安全监控提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) introduce new opportunities for safety monitoring through their Chain of Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, CoT is not always faithful to the model's final output, undermining its reliability as a monitoring tool. To address this, we investigate the hidden representations of LRMs to determine whether future behavior can be predicted from prompt and CoT representations. By evaluating a probe at each generated token, we construct a probe trajectory, the continuous evolution of a concept's probability across the reasoning process. We find that future model behavior is more distinguishable when examined over the full trajectory than from a single static prediction. To characterize these temporal dynamics, we extract signal-processing features that capture volatility, trend, and steady-state behavior, significantly improving the separation of future model states. We also present two methodological insights. First, template-based training data achieves near-parity with dynamically generated model responses, eliminating the need for a costly initial inference and labeling. Second, the choice of pooling operation is critical: average-pooling and last-token methods collapse to near-random performance, while max-pooling achieves up to 95% AUROC and yields stable probe trajectories. Using four datasets and four reasoning models across the domains of safety and mathematics, we demonstrate that trajectory features encode task-specific dynamics that improve outcome separability. These findings establish probe trajectories as a complementary framework for monitoring LRM behavior.
  Warning: This article contains potentially harmful content.

</details>


### [174] [Readers make targeted regressions to plausible errors in reanalysis of "noisy-channel garden-path" sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18563)
*Thomas Hikaru Clark,Roger Levy,Edward Gibson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过眼动实验研究读者如何处理"噪声通道花园小径句"——这类句子表面语法正确但后期出现违背预期的错误，读者需推断输入中存在错误而非重新分析句法。研究发现读者会进行目标性回视，即眼睛回看那些可能包含错误的区域，其模式与带重分析的噪声通道处理模型的 posterior 推断一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类在语言理解过程中如何增量式地推断语言输入的含义，特别是当句子出现晚期违背预期的错误时，读者如何通过推断输入中存在错误（而非重新分析句法结构）来理解句子。

Method: 使用眼动追踪技术研究读者阅读"噪声通道花园小径句"时的阅读动态，分析回视（regressions）的模式，并与噪声通道处理模型的后验推断进行对比。

Result: 发现读者存在目标性回视行为——眼睛回看那些可能是错误来源的区域，这种模式与带重分析的噪声通道模型的后验推断一致。

Conclusion: 这些发现对噪声通道语言理解理论和阅读动态的信息论解释具有重要意义。

Abstract: A key question in psycholinguistics is how inferences about the meaning of linguistic input unfold incrementally a comprehender's mind. In this work, we study reading dynamics for ``noisy-channel garden-path'' sentences, which temporarily appear well-formed but feature late-appearing violations of expectation that can be resolved not by inferring an alternative syntactic structure, but by inferring the presence of an error. We find evidence for targeted regressions -- eye movements towards regions that are promising loci of possible errors in light of later-arriving information, showing patterns consistent with the posterior inferences of a model of noisy-channel processing with reanalysis. We discuss the implications of these findings for theories of noisy-channel language comprehension and information-theoretic explanations of reading dynamics.

</details>


### [175] [LongMINT: Evaluating Memory under Multi-Target Interference in Long-Horizon Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18565)
*Hyunji Lee,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Joykirat Singh,Zaid Khan,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LongMINT基准，包含15.6K个问答对，上下文平均长度138.8K tokens（最长1.8M），评估7种记忆增强系统在长时、高干扰场景下的表现，发现平均准确率仅27.9%，尤其在多证据聚合推理任务上表现糟糕，主要瓶颈在于检索和记忆构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准聚焦于静态、独立的记忆检索，未能捕捉真实世界中信息随时间动态更新、记忆间相互干扰的场景，亟需一个能评估智能体在长时间跨度下处理信息更新与记忆干扰能力的基准。

Method: 构建LongMINT基准，包含四种领域（状态追踪、多轮对话、维基百科修订、GitHub提交），设计两类问题（单目标召回和多目标聚合），评估7种系统（vanilla长上下文LLM、RAG、记忆增强智能体框架）的性能。

Result: 7种系统平均准确率仅27.9%，多目标聚合任务表现更差；性能主要受限于检索和记忆构建；系统难以召回被后续上下文修订或干扰的早期事实，且性能随干预更新次数增加而下降。

Conclusion: 当前记忆增强系统在长时、高干扰的真实场景下表现严重不足，亟需改进检索机制和记忆构建策略，以提升对动态更新信息的鲁棒召回与聚合推理能力。

Abstract: Real-world agents operate over long and evolving horizons, where information is repeatedly updated and may interfere across memories, requiring accurate recall and aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of information. However, existing benchmarks focus on static, independent recall and fail to capture these dynamic interactions between evolving memories. In this paper, we study how current memory-augmented agents perform in realistic, interference-heavy, long-horizon settings across diverse domains and question types. We introduce LongMINT (Long-Horizon Memory under INTerference), a benchmark featuring (1) long, highly interconnected contexts with frequently updated information that induces substantial interference, (2) diverse domains (state tracking, multi-turn dialogue, Wikipedia revisions, and GitHub commits), enabling evaluation of domain generalization, and (3) diverse question types that assess robustness to interference, including (i) single-target recall tasks requiring retrieval of a specific target from long contexts, and (ii) multi-target aggregation tasks requiring reasoning over multiple relevant pieces of information. Overall, LongMINT has 15.6k question-answering pairs over long-horizon contexts averaging 138.8k tokens and extending up to 1.8M tokens per instance. We evaluate 7 representative systems, including vanilla long-context LLMs, RAG, and memory-augmented agent frameworks. Across all systems, we observe consistently low performance (avg. 27.9% accuracy), especially on questions requiring aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of evidence. Our analysis shows that performance is primarily limited by retrieval and memory construction. Furthermore, current memory systems struggle to recall and reason over earlier facts that are later revised or interfered with by subsequent context, with performance degrading as the number of intervening updates increases.

</details>


### [176] [GUT-IS: A Data-Driven Approach to Integrating Constructs and Their Relations in Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18567)
*Maximilian Reinhardt,Jonas Scharfenberger,Burkhardt Funk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合任务自适应文本嵌入与聚类的方法，用于整合结构方程模型中的构念定义，并通过显式权衡语义纯度与聚类简洁性的损失函数选择最优解，以解决IS研究中构念定义不一致阻碍知识积累的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构方程模型在IS研究中广泛应用，但构念定义的不一致严重阻碍了知识的累积发展，亟需一种系统化的方法来实现构念的整合与统一建模。

Method: 采用任务自适应文本嵌入与聚类技术生成构念分组的候选集，然后通过一个显式权衡语义纯度与聚类数量简洁性的损失函数，从候选集中选择最优的构念分组方案。

Result: 在IS领域的两个数据集上对所提方法进行了实证评估与探索，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过显式化纯度与简洁性之间的权衡，能够分析当优先级从纯度转向简洁性时，构念分组及其关系如何变化，为结构方程模型的统一整合提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Structural equation modeling is widely used in IS research. However, inconsistent construct definitions impede the cumulative development of knowledge. In this work, we present an approach that aims at the integration of structural equation models into a unified model: We use a combination of task-adapted text embeddings and clustering to produce a candidate set of construct groupings. Subsequently, we select the optimal solution using a loss function that explicitly trades off semantic purity and parsimony in the number of clusters. By making this trade-off explicit, our approach allows to analyze how construct groupings and their relations change as one shifts the priority from purity to parsimony. Empirically, we evaluate and explore the proposed methodology on two datasets from the IS domain.

</details>


### [177] [MA$^{2}$P: A Meta-Cognitive Autonomous Intelligent Agents Framework for Complex Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18572)
*Dingyi Zhang,Ziqing Zhuang,Linhai Zhang,Ziyang Gao,Deyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MA$^{2}$P，一个基于元认知的自主智能体框架，通过多智能体架构协调感知管理、心理状态推断、策略执行、记忆维护和性能评估，并设计元认知配置器从结构化知识库中选择元策略以缓解跨领域性能差异，实验证明该方法在复杂说服任务中取得了更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说服对话系统在复杂场景下常生成通用或弱相关的回复，且大语言模型在不同领域的表现差异较大，缺乏对说服对象潜在心理状态（信念、欲望等）的有效推断和针对性策略生成能力。

Method: 提出MA$^{2}$P元认知自主智能体框架，包含：(1) 多智能体架构，协调感知管理、心理状态推断、策略执行、记忆维护和性能评估模块；(2) 元认知配置器，在对话开始时从结构化知识库中选择合适的元策略，指导后续推理和规划。

Result: 实验结果表明，MA$^{2}$P在说服成功率上超越了基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过元认知机制和多智能体协作，MA$^{2}$P有效解决了跨领域性能差异问题，在复杂说服任务中表现出更优的说服效果。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue generation plays a vital role in decision-making, negotiation, counseling, and behavior change, yet it remains a challenging problem. In complex persuasion where the persuadee's internal states are not expressed clearly, the persuader must interpret responses, infer the persuadee's latent mental states (e.g., beliefs and desires), and translate them into targeted, strategy-consistent actions; however, current approaches often produce generic or weakly grounded responses even when such cues are identified. Moreover, although large language models (LLMs) can generate persuasive content, their performance varies substantially across domains due to uneven knowledge coverage and limited reasoning generalization. To address these challenges, we propose MA$^{2}$P, a meta-cognitive autonomous intelligent agent framework for complex persuasion. Specifically, we develop an autonomous multi-agent architecture that coordinates perception management, mental-state inference, strategy execution, memory maintenance, and performance evaluation. To mitigate cross-domain performance variation, we further design a meta-cognitive configurator that selects an appropriate meta-strategy from a structured knowledge base at the outset, thereby guiding subsequent reasoning and planning. Experimental results show that our approach achieves a higher persuasion success rate than baselines.

</details>


### [178] [Language-Switching Triggers Take a Latent Detour Through Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18646)
*Francis Kulumba,Wissam Antoun,Théo Lasnier,Benoît Sagot,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别并解析了8B参数自回归语言模型中语言切换后门攻击的内部电路机制，该后门由三词拉丁触发词（9个token）将英语输出重定向为法语。电路分为三个阶段：早期层注意力头将触发词信息汇聚到最后位置；中层信号在正交于模型自然语言身份方向的子空间中传播；最终层MLP将潜在信号转换为法语logits。整个电路通过单个位置的串行瓶颈流动，破坏该位置可完全消除触发效果但会损害模型能力。这种正交潜在编码意味着在中间表示中搜索类语言信号的防御措施将完全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管后门攻击对语言模型构成日益严重的安全威胁，但其触发序列劫持模型计算的内部机制仍缺乏深入理解。现有研究大多关注攻击方法本身，而对后门如何在模型内部电路层面实现的具体机理尚不清楚，这阻碍了针对性防御策略的发展。

Method: 采用电路分析方法，对8B参数自回归语言模型中的语言切换后门进行逆向工程。通过定位注意力头、MLP层及信息流路径，将后门电路分解为三个阶段：早期注意力头的分布式组合、中层正交子空间传播、最终层MLP转换，并识别出单位置的串行瓶颈。

Result: 揭示了后门电路的三阶段工作机制，发现信号通过正交于模型自然语言身份方向的潜在子空间传播，且存在单个位置的串行瓶颈——破坏该位置可完全消除后门触发效果，但同时会损害模型正常能力。

Conclusion: 语言切换后门可通过巧妙利用模型内部表示的正交子空间实现隐蔽执行，传统基于中间表示搜索语言信号的防御方法对此类后门无效。这提醒我们需要开发更鲁棒的后门检测与防御机制。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on language models pose a growing security concern, yet the internal mechanisms by which a trigger sequence hijacks model computations remain poorly understood. We identify a circuit underlying a language-switching backdoor in an 8B-parameter autoregressive language model, where a three-word Latin trigger (nine tokens) redirects English output to French. We decompose the circuit into three phases: (1) distributed attention heads at early layers compose the trigger tokens into the last sequence position; (2) the resulting signal propagates through mid-layers in a subspace orthogonal to the model's natural language-identity direction; (3) the MLP at the final layer converts this latent signal into French logits. The entire circuit flows through a serial bottleneck at a single position: corrupting that position at any layer entirely mitigate the trigger but also hinder the model's capabilities. The orthogonal latent encoding suggests that defenses that search for language-like signals in intermediate representations would miss this trigger entirely.

</details>


### [179] [EnvFactory: Scaling Tool-Use Agents via Executable Environments Synthesis and Robust RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18703)
*Minrui Xu,Zilin Wang,Mengyi DENG,Zhiwei Li,Zhicheng Yang,Xiao Zhu,Yinhong Liu,Boyu Zhu,Baiyu Huang,Chao Chen,Heyuan Deng,Fei Mi,Lifeng Shang,Xingshan Zeng,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EnvFactory是一个自动化框架，通过从真实资源自主构建可执行工具环境并合成自然的多轮轨迹，解决了LLM工具使用中缺乏可扩展执行环境和真实训练数据的瓶颈，仅用85个环境即可显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工具使用方法依赖昂贵的真实API、易产生幻觉的模拟器或合成环境，且合成轨迹过于明确、类似指令序列而非自然人类意图，导致强化学习训练效果不佳。需要可扩展、鲁棒的执行环境和真实训练数据。

Method: EnvFactory包含两个核心模块：(1) 自主探索并验证来自真实资源的可执行状态化工具环境；(2) 通过拓扑感知采样和校准精炼，合成具有隐式意图的自然多轮轨迹。

Result: 仅用85个验证环境(覆盖7个领域)生成2,575条SFT和RL轨迹，在BFCLv3上提升+15%，MCP-Atlas上+8.6%，τ²-Bench和VitaBench上+6%，训练效率和下游性能均优于使用5倍以上环境的前沿方法。

Conclusion: EnvFactory通过完全自动化环境构建和轨迹合成，为Agentic RL提供了可扩展、可扩展且鲁棒的基础，证明了高质量环境比大量环境更关键。

Abstract: Equipping LLMs with tool-use capabilities via Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) is bottlenecked by two challenges: the lack of scalable, robust execution environments and the scarcity of realistic training data that captures implicit human reasoning. Existing approaches depend on costly real-world APIs, hallucination-prone LLM simulators, or synthetic environments that are often single-turn or depend on pre-collected documents. Moreover, synthetic trajectories are frequently over-specified, resembling instruction sequences rather than natural human intents, reducing their effectiveness for RL training. We introduce EnvFactory, a fully automated framework that addresses both challenges. EnvFactory autonomously explores and verifies stateful, executable tool environments from authentic resources, and synthesizes natural multi-turn trajectories through topology-aware sampling and calibrated refinement, producing grounded queries with implicit intents. Using only 85 verified environments across 7 domains, EnvFactory generates 2,575 SFT and RL trajectories. Despite using significantly fewer environments than prior work, which are often 5 times more, EnvFactory achieves superior training efficiency and downstream performance, improving Qwen3-series models by up to +15% on BFCLv3, +8.6% on MCP-Atlas, and +6% on conversational benchmarks including $τ^2$-Bench and VitaBench. By fully automating both environment construction and trajectory synthesis, EnvFactory provides a scalable, extensible, and robust foundation for Agentic RL.

</details>


### [180] [Predictable Confabulations: Factual Recall by LLMs Scales with Model Size and Topic Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18732)
*Matthew L. Smith,Jonathan P. Shock,Samuel T. Segun,Iyiola E. Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对38个模型、8900+学术参考文献的评估，发现事实性回忆质量遵循一种sigmoid函数，其自变量是模型参数量和训练数据中主题表征的对数线性组合。两个变量解释了16个稠密模型间60%的方差，在单个模型家族内可升至74-94%。该形式与一种基于叠加假说的解释一致：回忆由信噪比门控，信号强度随概念频率缩放，噪声基底随模型容量缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规模定律（scaling laws）只刻画了语言模型整体的性能趋势，但尚未建立关于事实性回忆（factual recall）与模型规模及训练数据组成之间关系的定量规律。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索模型参数、数据中主题出现频率与事实回忆质量之间的可预测关系。

Method: 在38个模型上使用自动化引文验证系统对超过8,900条学术参考文献进行评估；提取模型参数量与训练数据中主题表征两个变量，拟合其对事实回忆质量的sigmoid函数；分析16个稠密模型（来自4个模型家族）的方差解释度，并在单个家族内进一步检验。

Result: 回忆质量遵循以模型参数量与主题表征的对数线性组合为自变量的sigmoid曲线；两变量解释跨家族模型60%的方差，在单个模型家族内解释度提升至74-94%；该模式与叠加假说（superposition）一致，即回忆由信噪比决定：信号随概念频率增强，噪声基底随模型容量增大。

Conclusion: 本文首次建立了事实回忆质量关于模型规模与训练数据频率的规模定律，揭示了回忆行为的信噪比门控机制，对理解大语言模型内部如何编码和提取事实知识具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: While scaling laws govern aggregate large language model performance, no scaling law has linked factual recall to both model size and training-data composition. We evaluated 38 models on over 8,900 scholarly references evaluated by an automated reference verification system. Recall quality follows a sigmoid in the log-linear combination of model parameter count and topic representation in training data. These two variables alone explain 60% of the variance across 16 dense models from four families, rising to 74-94% within individual families. The form matches a superposition-inspired account in which recall is gated by a signal-to-noise ratio: signal strength scales with concept frequency and the noise floor with model capacity.

</details>


### [181] [Code as Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18747)
*Xuying Ning,Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Zihao Li,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Mengting Ai,Zhining Liu,Ting-Wei Li,Lingjie Chen,Yanjun Zhao,Ke Yang,Bingxuan Li,Cheng Qian,Gaotang Li,Xiao Lin,Zhichen Zeng,Ruizhong Qiu,Sirui Chen,Yifan Sun,Xiyuan Yang,Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Chenyuan Yang,Dylan Zhang,Liri Fang,Zikun Cui,Yang Cao,Pan Chen,Dorothy Sun,Ren Chen,Mahesh Srinivasan,Nipun Mathur,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Pan Lu,Lingming Zhang,Tong Zhang,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"代码即智能体框架"（Code as Agent Harness）的统一视角，围绕框架接口、框架机制、框架扩展三个层次系统综述了以代码为核心的智能体基础设施，涵盖从规划、记忆、工具使用到多智能体协作等关键方法与应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体系统兴起背景下，代码不再仅是目标输出，而是逐渐成为智能体推理、行动、环境建模和执行验证的运作基板。作者希望通过"代码即智能体框架"这一统一视角，系统梳理这一范式转变，为可执行、可验证、有状态的AI智能体系统提供统一路线图。

Method: 围绕三个连接层次组织综述：(1)框架接口层——代码连接智能体的推理、行动与环境建模；(2)框架机制层——规划、记忆、工具使用支撑长周期执行，以及基于反馈的控制与优化；(3)框架扩展层——从单智能体扩展到多智能体场景，共享代码制品支持多智能体协调、审查与验证。并在各层次总结代表性方法和实际应用。

Result: 系统总结了代码作为智能体框架在编码助手、GUI/OS自动化、具身智能体、科学发现、个性化推荐、DevOps和企业工作流等领域的实际应用，并识别了开放挑战：超越最终任务成功的评估、不完整反馈下的验证、无回归的框架改进、多智能体间一致共享状态、安全关键行动的人工监督、以及多模态环境扩展。

Conclusion: 通过将代码置于智能体AI的核心位置，本文为构建可执行、可验证、有状态的AI智能体系统提供了统一的路线图，指出了智能体框架工程在未来需要重点攻克的关键挑战。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding and generating code, from competitive programming to repository-level software engineering. In emerging agentic systems, code is no longer only a target output. It increasingly serves as an operational substrate for agent reasoning, acting, environment modeling, and execution-based verification. We frame this shift through the lens of agent harnesses and introduce code as agent harness: a unified view that centers code as the basis for agent infrastructure. To systematically study this perspective, we organize the survey around three connected layers. First, we study the harness interface, where code connects agents to reasoning, action, and environment modeling. Second, we examine harness mechanisms: planning, memory, and tool use for long-horizon execution, together with feedback-driven control and optimization that make harness reliable and adaptive. Third, we discuss scaling the harness from single-agent systems to multi-agent settings, where shared code artifacts support multi-agent coordination, review, and verification. Across these layers, we summarize representative methods and practical applications of code as agent harness, spanning coding assistants, GUI/OS automation, embodied agents, scientific discovery, personalization and recommendation, DevOps, and enterprise workflows. We further outline open challenges for harness engineering, including evaluation beyond final task success, verification under incomplete feedback, regression-free harness improvement, consistent shared state across multiple agents, human oversight for safety-critical actions, and extensions to multimodal environments. By centering code as the harness of agentic AI, this survey provides a unified roadmap toward executable, verifiable, and stateful AI agent systems.

</details>


### [182] [DashAttention: Differentiable and Adaptive Sparse Hierarchical Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18753)
*Yuxiang Huang,Nuno M. T. Gonçalves,Federico Alvetreti,Lei Li,Xu Han,Edoardo M. Ponti,André F. T. Martins,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DashAttention提出了一种可微分且自适应稀疏的分层注意力机制，通过α-entmax变换替代传统的top-k操作，实现变长稀疏块选择并保持整个层级可微分，在75%稀疏度下达到与全注意力相当的准确率，并在高稀疏场景下优于NSA和InfLLMv2，同时Triton实现比FlashAttention-3推理速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层注意力方法（如NSA、InfLLMv2）使用top-k操作选择KV块存在两个问题：一是对任何查询假设固定数量的相关token，二是阻碍了稀疏阶段与稠密阶段之间的梯度流动。

Method: 利用自适应稀疏的α-entmax变换在第一阶段根据当前查询选择可变数量的块，为第二阶段的softmax注意力提供先验，使整个层级完全可微分。

Result: 在75%稀疏度下达到与全注意力相当的准确率；在高稀疏场景下优于NSA和InfLLMv2的帕累托边界；Triton实现相比FlashAttention-3在推理时有加速。

Conclusion: DashAttention通过可微分层级结构和自适应稀疏性，为长上下文建模提供了一种性价比高的策略。

Abstract: Current hierarchical attention methods, such as NSA and InfLLMv2, select the top-k relevant key-value (KV) blocks based on coarse attention scores and subsequently apply fine-grained softmax attention on the selected tokens. However, the top-k operation assumes the number of relevant tokens for any query is fixed and it precludes the gradient flow between the sparse and dense stages.
